# task.md — WORKSTREAM-01-CORE-SECURITY-AUTHORITY (Core Security Authority (HARD AUTHORITY))

Operator: master (one human, all six lanes). Coordinator: this lane's current A1 (see latest `HANDOFFS/`). Every task below is execution-ready with all 20 fields. A1 runs up to `MC_MAX_PARALLEL_A2` A2 tasks at once (AGENTS.md §3a), each in its own worktree with a fresh A2.

**Mission.** Own every freeze-closure component that mints, verifies, or revokes authority: canonical Rust codec and contract types, PostgreSQL authority roles/RLS and writer matrix, lease/fencing and Runner Control ingress, gate-input authority and evidence ingestion, effect intent/state and the two-copy witnessed journal, Approval Authority backend and credential lifecycle, patch custody, the credential-free Publication Planner plus the independent Git Publication Capability Gateway, Manual Resolution Authority, post-restore fresh security bootstrap, and coordination of the narrow external audit.

States: NOT_STARTED · DISPATCHED · RETURNED · IN_REVIEW · REPAIR · ACCEPTED · INTEGRATED · BLOCKED. RUN tasks additionally carry, per cell and per immutable run, the measured outcome (PASS/FAIL/INCONCLUSIVE, `claims/<id>/cells/<cell>/runs/<RUN_ID>/outcome.json`, written once) and the evidence lifecycle (NOT_RUN/RUNNING/SUBMITTED/ACCEPTED/REJECTED, `lifecycle.jsonl`); `cells/<cell>/current.json` names the current run.

## Ordering rules

- BUILD tasks may start after G0 + G1 when their PRECONDITIONS hold; use published doubles for anything another workstream has not INTEGRATED yet. Never let a lane idle waiting for another lane — use the double and record the pin.
- RUN tasks (`W01-RUN-*`) start only after G2 + G3 + their INTEGRATED build task(s) + integration milestone. Until then the harness is exercised as DRY_RUN inside BUILD tasks.
- Interfaces you provide (I-01, I-02, I-03, I-04, I-05, I-07, I-08, I-09, I-16) should be published early (doubles first) — consumers depend on them.
- Post-freeze production work is NOT in this file; see `POST_FREEZE/POST_FREEZE_BACKLOG.md` (not dispatched).

## BUILD tasks

### W01-T000-WORKSPACE-SCAFFOLD
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T000-WORKSPACE-SCAFFOLD
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Set up the empty Rust project for the whole security core: every crate, folder, database-migration folder, docker-compose file and CI job, with no logic inside. Later tasks then only fill in their own files, so five or more A2s can work at the same time without touching the same file. **Precisely:** Create the complete `ws01-core-security/` skeleton in one go, so every later W01 unit only edits its own `src/**`, tests and SQL files and never touches `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `compose.yaml` or CI. That covers: the Cargo workspace; every W01 crate, double, tool and claim-harness crate as a compiling empty skeleton with its final dependency list; the migration module layout; compose; CI; and the docs index.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Parallel A2s conflict only on shared files. When the scaffold owns all of them once, the other 51 units have disjoint write scopes and can run 5+ at a time (AGENTS.md §3a). ADR-001 modular monolith.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 00F frozen stack (Rust, Axum, Tokio, SQLx, Serde; PostgreSQL); ADR-001; 36 pinned lockfiles; 07 (one `mc_authority` schema, module-owned tables).
- **P0 CLAIMS:** none directly; enables all 13 W01 claims.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** I-14 test DB harness (W06); if not yet published, the PostgreSQL image is pinned by digest (recorded in memory.md) and switched to I-14 when it lands.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** Workspace layout below; `docs/MODULES.md` (crate → owning task table, copied from this task); `db/authority/MODULES` (ordered module list).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/{Cargo.toml,Cargo.lock,compose.yaml,.env.example,README.md,.gitignore}`, `ws01-core-security/docs/{MODULES.md,README.md}`, `ws01-core-security/crates/*/{Cargo.toml,src/lib.rs}`, `ws01-core-security/crates/*/src/*.rs` (empty declared modules only), `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-resolution/src/flows/mod.rs` (empty directory module only), `ws01-core-security/doubles/{wire-vector-table,lease,effects-journal,gate-authority,custody,runner-control}/v1/{Cargo.toml,src/lib.rs,README.md}`, `ws01-core-security/doubles/README.md`, `ws01-core-security/claims/*/harness/{Cargo.toml,src/main.rs}`, `ws01-core-security/claims/README.md`, `ws01-core-security/tools/codegen/{Cargo.toml,src/main.rs}`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/{MODULES,*/.keep}`, `.github/workflows/ws01-ci.yml`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `/rust-toolchain.toml`, `/deny.toml` (W06), `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/**`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** G0 (repo exists, ARCH_IMPORT_COMMIT in memory.md); G1 signed.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `Cargo.toml`; `Cargo.lock`; `compose.yaml`; `.env.example`; `README.md`; `.gitignore`; `docs/README.md`; `docs/MODULES.md`; `db/authority/MODULES`; `db/authority/{roles,base,lease,effects,journal,ingestion,gate,custody,approval,credential,resolution,publication,bootstrap}/.keep`; `crates/<21 crates>/{Cargo.toml,src/lib.rs,src/<declared module>.rs}`; `crates/mc-resolution/src/flows/mod.rs (empty directory module)`; `doubles/<6 doubles>/v1/{Cargo.toml,src/lib.rs,README.md}`; `doubles/README.md`; `claims/<13 claims>/harness/{Cargo.toml,src/main.rs}`; `claims/README.md`; `tools/codegen/{Cargo.toml,src/main.rs}`; `../.github/workflows/ws01-ci.yml`. **Steps:** 1. `Cargo.toml`: virtual workspace, `resolver = "2"`, `members = ["crates/*", "doubles/wire-vector-table/v1", "doubles/lease/v1", "doubles/effects-journal/v1", "doubles/gate-authority/v1", "doubles/custody/v1", "doubles/runner-control/v1", "claims/*/harness", "tools/*"]` — the six Rust doubles are listed by name because `doubles/aa-api/v1` and `doubles/resolution-api/v1` are OpenAPI-only directories with no Cargo.toml (audit F-3); `[workspace.dependencies]` pins exact versions (`=x.y.z`, latest stable on crates.io on the scaffold day, recorded in `docs/MODULES.md`) for: tokio (rt-multi-thread, macros, time, sync, net), axum, sqlx (postgres, runtime-tokio, tls-rustls, uuid, macros, migrate), serde, serde_json, uuid (v4, serde), sha2, hex, ed25519-dalek, p256 (ecdsa), base64, unicode-normalization, thiserror, tracing, tracing-subscriber, rustls, tokio-rustls, rcgen (dev), reqwest (rustls-tls, json, no default features), clap (derive), tempfile, proptest, async-trait. 2. Create these crates under `crates/`, each with `src/lib.rs` containing only `//! <one-line purpose> — PROTOTYPE — DISPOSABLE — NOT PRODUCTION` and the dependencies listed: mc-contracts (serde, serde_json, uuid, hex, sha2, thiserror); mc-cbor (thiserror, unicode-normalization); mc-wire (mc-contracts, mc-cbor, sha2, ed25519-dalek, uuid, thiserror, clap); mc-test-support (sqlx, tokio, uuid, ed25519-dalek, p256, serde_json); mc-authority-db (sqlx, tokio, uuid, thiserror, mc-contracts); mc-lease, mc-effects, mc-journal, mc-ingestion, mc-gate-authority, mc-custody, mc-approval-authority, mc-credential-lifecycle, mc-resolution, mc-plan-verifier, mc-publication-planner, mc-git-gateway, mc-bootstrap (each: mc-contracts, mc-wire, mc-authority-db, sqlx, tokio, uuid, thiserror, async-trait, tracing); add mc-lease to mc-effects, mc-ingestion and mc-gate-authority (their APIs name `LeaseRef`/`LeaseAuthority`); add mc-effects to mc-custody (`Arc<dyn EffectAuthority>`); mc-lease re-exports `pub use sqlx::PgConnection;` so every consumer names the same connection type; mc-negotiation (mc-contracts, mc-wire, thiserror); mc-runner-control (mc-lease, mc-negotiation, axum, tokio-rustls, rustls); mc-webauthn-verify (p256, sha2, base64, serde_json, thiserror); add `reqwest` to mc-git-gateway; add mc-journal to mc-effects; add mc-custody to mc-approval-authority and mc-plan-verifier; add mc-webauthn-verify to mc-approval-authority, mc-credential-lifecycle and mc-resolution; add mc-effects to mc-resolution, mc-git-gateway and mc-bootstrap; add mc-journal to mc-bootstrap; add mc-plan-verifier to mc-publication-planner and mc-git-gateway; add mc-approval-authority to mc-git-gateway; add mc-credential-lifecycle to mc-approval-authority and mc-bootstrap; add mc-ingestion to mc-gate-authority. Every crate except mc-contracts, mc-cbor, mc-authority-db and mc-test-support gets `mc-test-support` as a dev-dependency; every crate gets `proptest`; mc-runner-control also gets `rcgen` (dev). mc-test-support depends on mc-authority-db as an optional dependency behind feature `authority`. **Module declarations:** `src/lib.rs` declares every module that later tasks fill, each created as an empty file with a one-line doc comment, so no later task edits `lib.rs`: mc-custody (api, manifest, store, pins, snapshot, reader, retention, service); mc-approval-authority (api, prepare, display, challenge, http_prepare, finish, consume, anomaly, http_finish); mc-effects (api, store, dispatch, authority); mc-resolution (api, case, action, challenge, commit, http, flows — `flows` is a directory module `src/flows/mod.rs`); mc-credential-lifecycle (api, chain, registry, operations, recovery, break_glass); mc-bootstrap (api, fence, deny, bootstrap, unfreeze); mc-git-gateway (api, gateway, reconcile); mc-lease, mc-journal, mc-ingestion, mc-gate-authority, mc-plan-verifier, mc-publication-planner, mc-negotiation (api only; their single implementing task adds further modules). 3. Doubles under `doubles/<name>/v1/` (lib crates), each with tokio, serde_json and async-trait plus every crate whose interface it fakes: wire-vector-table (mc-wire), lease (mc-lease, sqlx), effects-journal (mc-effects, mc-journal), gate-authority (mc-ingestion, mc-gate-authority, mc-wire, ed25519-dalek), custody (mc-custody), runner-control (mc-lease, mc-negotiation, axum). The W02 reducer crate/double these later call are added by W01-T052, not here (W02's scaffold does not exist yet). 4. Claim harness binaries `claims/<claim>/harness/` for P0-06C, P0-06D, P0-07B, P0-08A, P0-09B, P0-09C, P0-09D, P0-09F, P0-09G, P0-09H, P0-10A, P0-10I, P0-10J: `src/main.rs` with `fn main() {}`, dependencies = the crates named in that claim's harness task plus clap, serde_json, tokio. 5. `tools/codegen` binary (serde_json, clap). 6. `db/authority/MODULES` lists migration modules in apply order, one per line: roles, base, lease, effects, journal, ingestion, gate, custody, approval, credential, resolution, publication, bootstrap; create each directory with `.keep`. Each module's migrations are `db/authority/<module>/NNNN_<name>.sql`, numbered inside the module only, so parallel units never collide. 7. `compose.yaml`: one PostgreSQL service, image pinned by digest, port from `${MC_WS01_PG_PORT}`, password from `${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}` (no default in git). 8. `.github/workflows/ws01-ci.yml` (job `ws01-ci`; change gate over `ws01-core-security/**` + itself); `permissions: contents: read`; jobs `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` with a PostgreSQL service container, `python3 ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/TEST_VECTORS/check_spec.py`; no `pull_request_target`. 9. `docs/MODULES.md`: the crate/double/harness → owning task table (from this task list), a **crate → imports table** built by reading every W01 task's "Exact public API" and READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES in task.md (each type a crate's public API names must come from a listed dependency; audit F-6), and the rule "only the scaffold edits Cargo.toml/Cargo.lock; a unit that needs a new crate returns BLOCKED with DEPENDENCY_REQUEST". 10. **Stub compile check:** in a scratch branch that is not committed, paste the W01-T011 signatures into the api.rs files and run `cargo check --workspace`; every missing dependency it reveals is added to this scaffold before RETURN (record the result in the RETURN). CI runs every cargo command with `--locked`. **Required-check rule (one operator, parallel auto-merge):** the workflow is named and has one job named `ws01-ci` (a required check on main), triggers on **every** pull request to main and every push to main (no workflow-level `paths:` filter, so it always reports), and its first step decides with `git diff --name-only <merge-base> HEAD` whether this lane's paths or the upstream paths listed here changed; if not, the remaining steps are skipped by `if:` and the job still reports success. Without this, auto-merge could merge a PR whose lane tests never ran.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** No secrets in compose/env example (placeholders only); CI token read-only; no `pull_request_target`; no crate contains logic. No workspace-level `rust-toolchain.toml`: the repository root pin (W06-T000) is the single toolchain source (rustup finds it from every workspace).
- **FAILURE CASES:** A skeleton crate missing a dependency that a later unit needs (that unit returns DEPENDENCY_REQUEST); a documentation-only directory matched as a Cargo member; CI triggering on other workstreams' paths; compose embedding a password.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo build --workspace` and `cargo test --workspace` green on the empty skeleton; CI green; `cargo metadata --format-version 1` lists exactly the members named in steps 2–5; after creating empty `doubles/aa-api/v1/` and `doubles/resolution-api/v1/` directories (as W01-T001/T002 will), `cargo metadata` still succeeds with the same members; the step-10 stub compile check passes. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `cargo build --workspace` exits 0; `cargo test --workspace` exits 0 (no tests yet); `cargo metadata --format-version 1 --no-deps | python3 -c` prints exactly 41 members (21 crates + 6 doubles + 13 harnesses + 1 tool); `docker compose config` shows no literal password; `with empty `doubles/aa-api/v1/` and `doubles/resolution-api/v1/` directories present, `cargo metadata` still lists the same 41 members`; `stub compile check: the W01-T011 signatures pasted into api.rs compile with `cargo check --workspace --locked` (scratch, not committed)`; `CI run green on the PR`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** A deliberately misformatted scratch file makes CI fail (shown in the return, then deleted); `git grep -n "POSTGRES_PASSWORD=" ws01-core-security` finds only the placeholder in `.env.example`.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** All 21 crates, 6 doubles, 13 harnesses and 1 tool exist and compile; no foreign members; module directories exist in the listed order; CI job `ws01-ci` always reports and skips heavy steps when nothing relevant changedly ws01 + itself.

### W01-T001-AA-API-OPENAPI
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T001-AA-API-OPENAPI
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write the API description (an OpenAPI file, no code) for the Approval Authority, the service that shows a human exactly what will be published and records their fingerprint/security-key approval. the W05 lane's approval web page and mock server are built from this file. **Precisely:** Write the I-07 Approval Authority API as an OpenAPI 3.1 file, before any backend code, so W05 can build its AA origin and mock server against it (W05-T013) in parallel with W01's backend.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Contract-first: W05's AA origin (W05-T017) used to wait for the whole AA backend; now it waits only for this spec.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 52 "Trusted display and ceremony", "Transaction and lifecycle", "Enrollment and recovery authority"; ENCODING WebAuthn rules; 16 AA origin.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09A, P0-09B, P0-09C.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** TrustedDisplayReceiptV1, ApprovalChallengeV2, WebAuthnCeremonyV1, HumanApprovalAssertionV2, ApprovalEnrollmentIntentV1, ApprovalEnrollmentChallengeV1, WebAuthnRegistrationCeremonyV1, ApprovalEnrollmentActionV1, ApprovalLifecycleChallengeV1, ApprovalLifecycleConsentV1, ApprovalEnrollmentAuthorizationV1, ApprovalCredentialBindingV1 (JSON projections, ENCODING.md).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `doubles/aa-api/v1/openapi.yaml` (I-07@v1) and `doubles/aa-api/v1/README.md` (versioning rule, error codes).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/doubles/aa-api/v1/{openapi.yaml,README.md}`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/SCHEMAS/**`, `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/52_*.md`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** G0, G1.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `doubles/aa-api/v1/openapi.yaml`; `doubles/aa-api/v1/README.md`. **Steps:** 1. Operations: `POST /aa/v1/plans/{plan_id}/prepare` → display model + TrustedDisplayReceiptV1 + ApprovalChallengeV2; `POST /aa/v1/challenges/{challenge_id}/finish` (body: WebAuthn assertion fields as base64url without padding) → HumanApprovalAssertionV2 or error; `POST /aa/v1/enrollment/intents`, `POST /aa/v1/enrollment/{intent_id}/challenge`, `POST /aa/v1/enrollment/{intent_id}/register`, `POST /aa/v1/lifecycle/{action_id}/challenge`, `POST /aa/v1/lifecycle/{action_id}/consent`, `GET /aa/v1/credentials` (current bindings, read-only). 2. Only `plan_id` comes from the URL. No display content or approval field is ever accepted from the browser (INV-11). 3. Schemas `$ref` the registry JSON schemas by exact contract name; no renamed or partial copies. 4. Error model: closed enum of codes (EXPIRED, REUSED, ORIGIN_MISMATCH, RP_ID_MISMATCH, UV_MISSING, SIGNATURE_INVALID, CREDENTIAL_STATE, PLAN_NOT_CURRENT, ELIGIBILITY, SCOPE) with HTTP status per code. 5. Security schemes: host-only session cookie + CSRF header; document "no CORS, no embedding".
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-10, INV-11: no endpoint lets the caller supply display content, plan digest or verdict.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Request body containing a plan digest; a schema copied and renamed instead of referenced.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** OpenAPI lint (`npx @redocly/cli lint` or `python3 -m openapi_spec_validator`) passes; every `$ref` resolves. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `npx -y @redocly/cli lint doubles/aa-api/v1/openapi.yaml` exits 0; `grep -nE 'plan_digest|display|verdict' doubles/aa-api/v1/openapi.yaml` shows no request-body property (A3 checks each hit is a response field). Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Grep shows no request schema with display/plan-digest/verdict fields.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** All 8 operations specified; error enum closed; refs exact; lint clean.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Lint output; file SHA-256.

### W01-T002-RESOLUTION-API-OPENAPI
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T002-RESOLUTION-API-OPENAPI
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write the API description (an OpenAPI file, no code) for the Resolution Authority, the service where a human resolves security cases the system could not decide on its own. the W05 lane's resolution page and mock server are built from this file. **Precisely:** Write the I-08 Resolution Authority API as an OpenAPI 3.1 file before any backend code, so W05 can build the resolution origin and its mock (W05-T014) in parallel.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Contract-first: W05's resolution origin (W05-T018) used to wait for the whole resolution backend.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 56 in full; 13 maintainer-oracle approval protocol; CONTRACT_AUTHORITY prior_state_digest rule.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-10J, P0-07C, P0-09H.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** ManualCaseStateV1, ManualResolutionActionV1, ResolutionChallengeV1, ManualResolutionV1, OracleTrustClassV1, EffectStateV1.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `doubles/resolution-api/v1/openapi.yaml` (I-08@v1) + `README.md`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/doubles/resolution-api/v1/{openapi.yaml,README.md}`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/SCHEMAS/**`, `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/56_*.md`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** G0, G1.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `doubles/resolution-api/v1/openapi.yaml`; `doubles/resolution-api/v1/README.md`. **Steps:** 1. Operations: `POST /res/v1/cases` (idempotent open: tenant, subject, kind, idempotency key); `GET /res/v1/cases/{case_id}` (canonical case + evidence, server-fetched); `POST /res/v1/cases/{case_id}/actions` (ManualResolutionActionV1 with expected case revision) → ResolutionChallengeV1; `POST /res/v1/challenges/{id}/finish` (WebAuthn assertion) → ManualResolutionV1. 2. Each of the seven doc-56 flows is identified by its exact registry pair `ManualResolutionActionV1` (kind, decision) per doc 56 flow, registry names exactly: untrusted agent oracle = (UNTRUSTED_ORACLE, PROVIDE_ORACLE); disputed finding = (DISPUTED_FINDING, REQUEST_FRESH_EVALUATION | REMAIN_BLOCKED); false-positive claim = (FALSE_POSITIVE, CREATE_POLICY_EXCLUSION); unsupported verification/C3 = (UNSUPPORTED_VERIFICATION, REQUEST_FRESH_EVALUATION | ACCEPT_RISK | REMAIN_BLOCKED); UNKNOWN effect with exact positive result = (EXTERNAL_EFFECT_UNCERTAINTY, CONFIRM_EFFECT); claimed nonexecution = (EXTERNAL_EFFECT_UNCERTAINTY, PROVE_TERMINAL_NONEXECUTION); uncertainty cannot be proved = (EXTERNAL_EFFECT_UNCERTAINTY, CLOSE_UNRESOLVED). APPROVAL_RECOVERY is not a doc-56 flow (credential recovery is W01-T036): the resolution authority rejects it fail-closed with NotAuthorized. REVOKE is accepted only where a flow above lists it (none in V2.1) — otherwise NotAuthorized. 3. Closed error enum: STALE_REVISION, EXPIRED, REUSED, NOT_AUTHORIZED, SEPARATION_OF_DUTY, CONFLICTING_PROOF, TERMINAL. 4. State in the doc that no operation can produce a PASS verdict.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-22; case content is fetched by the server, never accepted from the browser.
- **FAILURE CASES:** An action endpoint without expected revision (allows stale decisions).
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** OpenAPI lint passes; refs resolve. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `npx -y @redocly/cli lint doubles/resolution-api/v1/openapi.yaml` exits 0; `every mutating operation has a required `expected_revision` (A3 checks)`; `grep -n PASS` finds no enum value PASS in response schemas. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Grep shows no "PASS" value reachable in any response schema of a resolution.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** 4 operations; 7 flows; closed error enum; lint clean.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Lint output; SHA-256.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Mock server (W05-T014); backend (W01-T027, W01-T035).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Revision/CAS present on every mutation; no PASS path.

### W01-T003-RUNNER-CONTROL-PROTOCOL-SPEC
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T003-RUNNER-CONTROL-PROTOCOL-SPEC
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write the protocol description for Runner Control, the API the runner machines use to ask for work, keep their job alive and hand in results. the W03 lane builds the runner against this file. **Precisely:** Write the I-09 Runner Control protocol (routes, bodies, mTLS identity, negotiation handshake, error codes) as OpenAPI 3.1 plus a CBOR-body note, so W03 can build the runner client against it before the server exists.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** W03-T014 and the P0-08A/P0-06D integrations need a stable protocol early.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 10 Runner Control ingress; 20 runner protocol; 47 version rule; ENCODING version and extension rule; 07/29 lease predicate.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-08A, P0-06D.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** CapabilityProfileV1, AttemptBindingV2, AttemptEventV2, ProducerEventV2, SignedEnvelopeV1.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `doubles/runner-control/v1/openapi.yaml` + `PROTOCOL.md` (I-09@v1 spec).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/doubles/runner-control/v1/{openapi.yaml,PROTOCOL.md}`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/**`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** G0, G1.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `doubles/runner-control/v1/openapi.yaml`; `doubles/runner-control/v1/PROTOCOL.md`. **Steps:** 1. Routes: `POST /rc/v1/session` (authenticated negotiation: advertised sorted `(type, revision, registry_digest)` set → pinned set digest), `POST /rc/v1/poll`, `POST /rc/v1/accept`, `POST /rc/v1/heartbeat`, `POST /rc/v1/complete`, `POST /rc/v1/receipts`. 2. Signed records travel as canonical CBOR (`application/cbor`) inside the JSON envelope as lowercase hex; every message carries the pinned set digest. 3. Error enum = LeaseError of I-03 (Expired, WrongOwner, StaleGeneration, WrongEpoch, IllegalState, ClockUnhealthy, Conflict) plus NegotiationFailed, ProtocolChanged, Unauthenticated. 4. mTLS: the client certificate maps to one runner identity; no DB credential ever leaves the server.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Negotiation completes before any lease/authority issuance; no opaque extension forwarding (INV-18).
- **FAILURE CASES:** A route usable before the session is negotiated.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** OpenAPI lint passes. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `npx -y @redocly/cli lint doubles/runner-control/v1/openapi.yaml` exits 0; `every non-session route lists NegotiationFailed and ProtocolChanged responses (A3 checks)`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Every non-session route documents ProtocolChanged/NegotiationFailed responses.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** 6 routes; error enum aligned with I-03; lint clean.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Lint output; SHA-256.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Server (W01-T029), double (W01-T026), negotiation logic (W01-T023).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Ordering negotiation → authority; error alignment.

### W01-T004-CONTRACT-TYPES-CODEGEN
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T004-CONTRACT-TYPES-CODEGEN
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Generate Rust types for all 85 data records defined in the architecture, straight from the registry file, so every lane uses exactly the same names, fields and rules. This is the first thing the other workstreams wait for: do it as early as possible. **Precisely:** Generate the `mc-contracts` crate (I-01@v1): Rust types for all 85 registered contracts (serde JSON projection, registry field order, enum code tables), generated mechanically from `REGISTRY.json` by `tools/codegen`. **This is the program's critical path**: W02, W03 and W04 start their first real task after it. Dispatch it the moment T000 is ACCEPTED.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Every workstream needs the same canonical names, order and enums without local variants (ADR-024; Shared Contract Rule).
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** SCHEMAS/REGISTRY.json (exact names, order, primitives, enums, options/sets); ENCODING.md (u64/i64 as decimal strings in JSON, UUID lowercase hyphenated, bytes lowercase hex, option = 0/1-element array, sets sorted); 49 glossary (no aliases).
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-06A/B/C/D.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** REGISTRY.json (REGISTRY_SHA256 b516004c…); `*.schema.json`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** crate `mc-contracts`: `src/primitives.rs` (hand-written), `src/generated.rs` (generated, committed), one struct per contract, one enum per registered enum, `const TYPE_NAME`, `const FIELD_ORDER: &[&str]`, enum `code()`/`from_code()` = zero-based registry position, `REGISTRY_SHA256` constant checked at build time. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct U64Str(pub u64)`, `pub struct I64Str(pub i64)`, `pub struct Hex<const N: usize>(pub [u8; N])`, `pub struct Bytes(pub Vec<u8>)`, `pub struct GitPath(pub Vec<u8>)`, `pub struct Opt<T>(pub Option<T>)`, `pub struct SetOf<T>(Vec<T>)` (constructor `SetOf::new(Vec<T>) -> Result<Self, SetError>` sorts? NO: rejects unsorted/duplicates), each with Serialize/Deserialize per ENCODING.md; per contract `pub struct <Name> { … }` + `impl <Name> { pub const TYPE_NAME: &'static str; pub const FIELD_ORDER: &'static [&'static str]; }`; per enum `pub enum <Enum> { … }` + `fn code(&self) -> u64` + `fn from_code(u64) -> Option<Self>`; `pub const REGISTRY_SHA256: &str`; codegen CLI `codegen --registry <path> --out <path> [--check]`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-contracts/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-contracts/tests/**`, `ws01-core-security/tools/codegen/src/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/SCHEMAS/**`, `ARCHITECTURE/V2_1/TEST_VECTORS/wire-vectors.json`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T000 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-contracts/src/lib.rs (owned by this task: `pub mod primitives; pub mod generated; pub use generated::*;`)`; `crates/mc-contracts/src/primitives.rs`; `crates/mc-contracts/src/generated.rs (generated)`; `crates/mc-contracts/build.rs or tests/registry_hash.rs`; `crates/mc-contracts/tests/{primitives.rs,accept_vectors.rs,reject_vectors.rs,registry_hash.rs}`; `tools/codegen/src/main.rs`. **Steps:** 1. `primitives.rs`: `U64Str`/`I64Str` (string (de)serialization; reject leading zeros, plus, exponent, overflow, JSON numbers), `Uuid` lowercase-hyphenated only, `Hex<N>` exact-length lowercase hex (digest/bytes32/bytes64), `Bytes` variable hex, `GitPath`, `Opt<T>` as `[]`/`[x]`, `SetOf<T>` validated sorted+unique on deserialize. 2. `tools/codegen`: read REGISTRY.json, emit `generated.rs` with `#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]` on every struct; `type` and `wire_revision` validated equal to the registered name and 1; no `Default` derives. 3. `build.rs` or a test asserts SHA-256(REGISTRY.json) == REGISTRY_SHA256. 4. CI freshness test: running codegen produces byte-identical `generated.rs`. 5. Vector tests: every ACCEPT projection deserializes and re-serializes to an equal JSON value; every projection-level REJECT vector rejects.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-18; no aliases (49); no defaults for missing fields; no JSON Number for u64/i64.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Silent default on a missing field; numeric JSON accepted for u64; enum order drift; alias type names.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p mc-contracts`; `cargo run -p codegen -- --check`; clippy/fmt clean. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `primitives::u64_rejects_json_number`; `primitives::u64_rejects_leading_zero`; `primitives::u64_rejects_plus_and_exponent`; `primitives::u64_rejects_overflow`; `primitives::hex_rejects_uppercase`; `primitives::hex_rejects_wrong_length`; `primitives::uuid_rejects_uppercase`; `primitives::opt_rejects_two_elements`; `primitives::set_rejects_unsorted`; `primitives::set_rejects_duplicate`; `accept_vectors::all_85_projections_round_trip`; `reject_vectors::projection_level_rejects_all_reject`; `reject_vectors::unknown_field_rejected`; `reject_vectors::null_rejected`; `reject_vectors::wrong_type_rejected`; `reject_vectors::wrong_wire_revision_rejected`; `registry_hash::registry_sha256_matches`; `cargo run -p codegen -- --check` exits 0. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Unknown field, `null`, numeric u64, leading-zero decimal, wrong `type`/`wire_revision`, unsorted/duplicate set, 2-element option, uppercase hex — each rejected by a named test.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** 85 types; 85/85 ACCEPT projections round-trip; all projection-level REJECT vectors reject; regeneration byte-identical; no hand edits in `generated.rs`.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test counts per vector class; codegen command; `generated.rs` SHA-256. A1 then sends INTERFACE_NOTICE I-01@v1 to W02–W06.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** CBOR (T005/T012); semantic authority checks.
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Primitive mapping vs ENCODING.md; deny_unknown_fields everywhere; enum positions; no aliases.

### W01-T005-CBOR-CORE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T005-CBOR-CORE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write a tiny, strict CBOR encoder/decoder (CBOR is the compact binary format all signed records use). It must reject every malformed or non-canonical input before allocating memory. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-cbor`: a minimal deterministic CBOR encoder/decoder for exactly the subset ENCODING.md allows, with every rejection class enforced before allocation. It has no knowledge of contract types (generic values: unsigned/negative int, byte string, text string, definite array, boolean false/true — ENCODING.md "Boolean is exactly CBOR false/true"; audit F-4).
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Splitting the codec lets this run in parallel with T004; T012 then maps contracts onto it.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** ENCODING.md: definite-length arrays only; shortest integer encoding; no maps/tags/floats/null/undefined/indefinite; record ≤16 MiB, nesting ≤32, arrays ≤65,536 (reject before allocation); text must already be NFC (reject, never normalize).
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-06A/B (W06), P0-06C.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** none (byte-level).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc_cbor::{Value, encode(&Value) -> Vec<u8>, decode(&[u8]) -> Result<Value, CborError>}`; `CborError` variants: Truncated, Trailing, NonMinimal, ForbiddenMajor, Indefinite, Bound, Nesting, Nfc, Utf8. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub enum Value { Unsigned(u64), Negative(u64), Bytes(Vec<u8>), Text(String), Array(Vec<Value>), Bool(bool) }` (Bool = exactly CBOR false 0xf4 / true 0xf5, ENCODING.md; every other major-7 byte — null, undefined, floats, other simple values — is ForbiddenMajor; audit F-4); `pub fn encode(v: &Value) -> Vec<u8>`; `pub fn decode(b: &[u8]) -> Result<Value, CborError>`; `pub struct Limits { pub max_record: usize, pub max_depth: usize, pub max_array: usize }` with `Limits::ENCODING_MD` = 16 MiB / 32 / 65,536 and `decode_with(b, &Limits)`; `pub enum CborError { Truncated, Trailing, NonMinimal, ForbiddenMajor, Indefinite, Bound, Nesting, Nfc, Utf8 }`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-cbor/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-cbor/tests/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** ENCODING.md. **Independence rule:** never open `ws05-web-approval-ux/packages/wire-ts/**`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T000 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-cbor/src/lib.rs (owned)`; `crates/mc-cbor/src/{value.rs,encode.rs,decode.rs,error.rs}`; `crates/mc-cbor/tests/{roundtrip.rs,reject.rs,long_random.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Hand-written encoder/decoder (~300 lines); no general CBOR crate. 2. Decoder checks declared length ≤ remaining bytes and the bounds before any allocation. 3. Text: valid UTF-8 and `is_nfc` true, else Nfc error. 4. Trailing bytes after the top-level item → Trailing. 5. Property tests: decode(encode(v)) == v; encoding is unique per value.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-18 fail-closed; no normalization of malformed input.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Non-minimal integer accepted; huge length allocates; trailing byte ignored.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p mc-cbor`; proptest round-trip; a long random-input decode test (`#[ignore]`, proptest with arbitrary byte vectors, run explicitly for 10 minutes) with no panic (cases executed recorded). **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `roundtrip::proptest_decode_encode_identity`; `roundtrip::encoding_is_unique`; `roundtrip::bool_false_is_f4_and_true_is_f5`; `reject::other_simple_values`; `reject::truncated`; `reject::trailing_byte`; `reject::non_minimal_int`; `reject::float`; `reject::tag`; `reject::map`; `reject::null_and_undefined`; `reject::indefinite_length`; `reject::length_exceeds_remaining_no_alloc`; `reject::nesting_33`; `reject::array_over_65536`; `reject::record_over_16mib`; `reject::non_nfc_text`; `reject::invalid_utf8`; `long_random::no_panic_10_minutes (#[ignore], run with --ignored)`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** One named test per CborError variant, plus: float, tag, map, null, undefined, every simple value other than false/true, indefinite length, nesting 33, length > remaining. Positive: `false` encodes as 0xf4 and `true` as 0xf5 and both round-trip.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Every rejection class covered; no panic in the long random-input run; no forbidden major type reachable.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test counts; long-run duration and case count.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Contract mapping, digests, signatures (T012).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Rejection before allocation; minimal-integer rule; no normalization.

- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T000 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `doubles/wire-vector-table/v1/src/lib.rs`; `doubles/wire-vector-table/v1/tests/lookup.rs`; `doubles/wire-vector-table/v1/README.md`. **Steps:** 1. Embed the vector file with `include_str!` and verify its SHA-256 against the manifest value. 2. Index ACCEPT vectors by (type, canonical JSON of the projection). 3. Return bytes and sha256 exactly as committed.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Never computes encodings itself; unknown input → DoubleUnsupported, never a guess.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Returning bytes for a projection that differs in one field.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p wire-vector-table`: all 85 ACCEPT lookups return the committed values. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `lookup::all_85_accept_vectors_exact`; `lookup::one_field_changed_is_unsupported`; `lookup::unknown_type_is_unsupported`; `lookup::vector_file_hash_matches`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** One-field-changed projection → DoubleUnsupported.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** 85/85 lookups exact; unknown → DoubleUnsupported.

### W01-T007-DB-ROLES-SCHEMA
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T007-DB-ROLES-SCHEMA
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Create the PostgreSQL security foundation: one database role per kind of writer, row-level security so one tenant can never see another's data, and the core gate tables. Every other database task builds on these roles. **Precisely:** Implement the PostgreSQL authority framework: the offline migration owner, all non-owner NOINHERIT NOBYPASSRLS runtime roles, schema `mc_authority` with the representative gate-critical tables (FORCE RLS, composite tenant keys and FKs, per-role grants from doc 07), the module-ordered migration runner, and `mc-authority-db` (tenant context set only from a verified assertion).
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** 07/ADR-013/ADR-019 make tenant isolation and writer separation structural; every other DB unit adds its own module tables on top.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 07 "Authority and DB-write matrix", "Grants and tenant isolation"; 18 relational constraints; ADR-013/019; INV-06, INV-15; a caller-set tenant GUC is not authentication.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** foundation for P0-10A, P0-07B.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** ScopeV1; doc 07 matrix.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `db/authority/roles/*.sql`, `db/authority/base/*.sql`; `mc_authority_db::{migrate(pool) /* applies MODULES in order */, TenantContext::from_verified(assertion) , with_tenant(tx, ctx)}`; role framework documented in `db/authority/roles/README.md` for other workstreams. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub async fn migrate(pool: &PgPool, modules_file: &Path) -> Result<(), DbError>`; `pub struct VerifiedTenantAssertion { pub tenant_id: Uuid, pub workload_id: Uuid, pub assertion_digest: [u8; 32] }` (constructed only by an authenticating caller); `pub struct TenantContext(..)` with `TenantContext::from_verified(&VerifiedTenantAssertion) -> Self`; `pub async fn with_tenant(tx: &mut Transaction<'_, Postgres>, ctx: &TenantContext) -> Result<(), DbError>`; `pub enum DbError { Migration(String /* file name only */), TenantMismatch, Sqlx }`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/db/authority/roles/**`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/base/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-authority-db/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-authority-db/tests/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** I-14 test DB harness.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T000 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `db/authority/roles/0001_roles.sql`; `db/authority/roles/README.md`; `db/authority/base/0001_schema.sql`; `db/authority/base/0002_tables.sql`; `db/authority/base/0003_rls_and_grants.sql`; `db/authority/base/0004_tenant_context.sql`; `crates/mc-authority-db/src/lib.rs (owned)`; `crates/mc-authority-db/src/{migrate.rs,tenant.rs,error.rs}`; `crates/mc-authority-db/tests/{roles.rs,grants.rs,rls.rs,tenant_context.rs,migrate.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Roles exactly: ordinary_api, runner_control, evidence_ingest_writer, verification_ingest_writer, verification_set_writer, gate_writer, policy_writer, candidate_writer, projection_writer, trust_writer, revocation_writer, manifest_writer, eligibility_writer, approval_writer, custody_writer, plan_writer, publication_writer, effect_writer, budget_writer, dependency_writer, resolution_writer, readonly — non-owner, NOINHERIT, NOBYPASSRLS, no SET ROLE paths. 2. Base tables: evaluation, gate_decision, analysis_completion_receipt, evidence_receipt, verification_receipt, verification_set, policy_version + pointer, trust_bundle + pointer, revocation, current_candidate pointer, expected_manifest — `tenant_id` in every PK/FK, FORCE RLS, grants per doc 07 (insert-only where immutable). 3. Tenant context: a SECURITY DEFINER function with fixed `search_path` receives the verified assertion and sets a transaction-local tenant; mismatch rejects. 4. `migrate()` reads `db/authority/MODULES` and applies each module directory in order.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-15; no runtime role owns tables or has BYPASSRLS; SECURITY DEFINER only with fixed search_path and narrow EXECUTE.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Pooled connection leaks the previous tenant; FK allows cross-tenant reference.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p mc-authority-db` using a fresh database per test (`mc-test-support` not yet available: use `#[sqlx::test]`); grants test per role × table. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `roles::all_22_roles_exist_nologin_noinherit_nobypassrls`; `roles::no_runtime_role_owns_tables`; `roles::set_role_between_runtime_roles_fails`; `grants::matrix_matches_doc07 (table-driven: role × table × op)`; `grants::ordinary_api_cannot_insert_gate_decision`; `rls::force_rls_on_every_base_table`; `rls::runtime_role_cannot_disable_rls`; `rls::cross_tenant_select_returns_zero`; `rls::cross_tenant_fk_rejected`; `tenant_context::mismatched_assertion_rejected`; `tenant_context::pooled_connection_does_not_leak_tenant`; `migrate::modules_applied_in_order_on_empty_db`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** `SET ROLE` between runtime roles fails; `ALTER TABLE … DISABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` by a runtime role fails; ordinary_api INSERT into gate_decision fails.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Role list exact; FORCE RLS on every base table; grants match doc 07 row by row; migrations apply cleanly on an empty DB.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Grant matrix (`\dp` output); test report. A1 sends INTERFACE_NOTICE "role framework v1" to W02–W06.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** P0-10A harness (T013); module tables of other units.
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Grants vs doc 07; GUC-not-authentication; SECURITY DEFINER hygiene.

### W01-T008-TEST-SUPPORT
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T008-TEST-SUPPORT
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write the shared test helpers: each test gets its own fresh database, random test keys, and helpers to read the database clock. This lets many A2s run tests at the same time on one machine without breaking each other. **Precisely:** Build `mc-test-support`, the shared dev-only helpers every later W01 unit uses so parallel A2s never collide: a fresh uniquely named database per test, TEST-labeled keys, DB-clock helpers and fixture loaders.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Parallel A2s share one PostgreSQL; fixed DB names or keys make tests fail randomly (AGENTS.md §3a).
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 42 test evidence rules; 07 (DB clock is the authority clock).
- **P0 CLAIMS:** none (enabler).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `DATABASE_URL` from `.env.local`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `TestDb::fresh().await` (creates `mc_test_<random>` DB, runs `mc_authority_db::migrate` when the `authority` feature is on, drops it on drop); `test_keys::ed25519(role: &str)` / `test_keys::p256()` (random per test, label TEST); `db_clock::now(&pool)` (`clock_timestamp()`), `db_clock::sleep_until(&pool, t)`; `fixtures::load(path)` with SHA-256 check. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct TestDb { pub pool: PgPool, pub name: String }` with `TestDb::fresh().await -> TestDb` (drops DB on Drop); `pub mod keys { pub fn ed25519(role: &str) -> TestEd25519; pub fn p256() -> TestP256; }` (each `.label() == "TEST"`); `pub mod db_clock { pub async fn now_us(pool: &PgPool) -> i64; pub async fn sleep_until_us(pool: &PgPool, t: i64); }`; `pub mod fixtures { pub fn load(path: &Path, sha256_hex: &str) -> Vec<u8>; }`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-test-support/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-test-support/tests/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-authority-db` (feature-gated, may be the empty skeleton).
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T000 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-test-support/src/lib.rs (owned)`; `crates/mc-test-support/src/{testdb.rs,keys.rs,db_clock.rs,fixtures.rs}`; `crates/mc-test-support/tests/{testdb.rs,keys.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. TestDb with a random suffix; cleanup on drop and on panic. 2. Keys generated per test, never written to disk. 3. Clock helpers read only `clock_timestamp()`. 4. The `authority` feature wires `migrate()` once T007 lands (compile-time optional).
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** No fixed secrets; nothing labeled other than TEST.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Two parallel test runs creating the same DB name; leaked DBs after a panic.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p mc-test-support` run twice concurrently in two shells without failure. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `testdb::two_testdbs_have_different_names`; `testdb::db_dropped_after_test`; `testdb::db_dropped_after_panic`; `keys::keys_are_random_and_labeled_test`; `manual: run `cargo test -p mc-test-support` in two shells at once; both pass`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.

### W01-T009-WEBAUTHN-VERIFY
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T009-WEBAUTHN-VERIFY
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write one library that checks WebAuthn (fingerprint/security-key) logins and approvals exactly as the standard requires. The approval, credential and resolution services all use this single checker instead of three separate ones. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-webauthn-verify`, one shared verification library used by the AA, credential lifecycle and resolution units: parse and verify `webauthn.get` assertions and `webauthn.create` registrations (attestation "none" only) exactly per ENCODING.md and doc 52.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Three authorities verify ceremonies; one reviewed implementation avoids three subtly different ones (G-055 critical parser).
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** ENCODING WebAuthn rules (challenge = D(…) as base64url without padding; ES256 over authData || SHA-256(clientDataJSON)); 52 ceremony checks; 36 focused review of crypto parsers.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09B, P0-09C, P0-10J.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** raw bytes only (authenticatorData, clientDataJSON, signature, public key COSE/SEC1).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `verify_assertion(expected: &ExpectedAssertion { challenge: [u8;32], origin: &str, rp_id: &str, require_uv: true, credential_public_key, prior_sign_count }, raw) -> Result<VerifiedAssertion { sign_count, flags }, WebAuthnError>`; `verify_registration(expected, raw) -> Result<VerifiedRegistration { credential_id, public_key }, WebAuthnError>`; WebAuthnError: Type, Challenge, Origin, CrossOrigin, RpIdHash, UserPresent, UserVerified, Signature, Counter, Format, Attestation. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct ExpectedAssertion<'a> { pub challenge: [u8; 32], pub origin: &'a str, pub rp_id: &'a str, pub credential_public_key: &'a [u8], pub prior_sign_count: u32 }` (UV always required, no field to disable it); `pub struct RawAssertion { pub authenticator_data: Vec<u8>, pub client_data_json: Vec<u8>, pub signature: Vec<u8>, pub credential_id: Vec<u8>, pub user_handle: Option<Vec<u8>> }`; `pub fn verify_assertion(e: &ExpectedAssertion, r: &RawAssertion) -> Result<VerifiedAssertion, WebAuthnError>`; `pub struct VerifiedAssertion { pub sign_count: u32, pub counter_anomaly: bool }`; `pub struct ExpectedRegistration<'a> { pub challenge: [u8; 32], pub origin: &'a str, pub rp_id: &'a str }`; `pub fn verify_registration(e, raw: &RawRegistration) -> Result<VerifiedRegistration, WebAuthnError>`; `pub struct VerifiedRegistration { pub credential_id: Vec<u8>, pub public_key_sec1: Vec<u8> }`; `pub enum WebAuthnError { Type, Challenge, Origin, CrossOrigin, RpIdHash, UserPresent, UserVerified, Signature, Counter, Format, Attestation }`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-webauthn-verify/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-webauthn-verify/tests/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** none. Cross-check fixtures from W05-T008 are used when available, never required.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T000 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-webauthn-verify/src/lib.rs (owned)`; `crates/mc-webauthn-verify/src/{client_data.rs,auth_data.rs,assertion.rs,registration.rs,error.rs}`; `crates/mc-webauthn-verify/tests/{assertion.rs,registration.rs,crosscheck.rs}`; `crates/mc-webauthn-verify/tests/support/soft_authenticator.rs`. **Steps:** 1. clientDataJSON: exact `type`, challenge byte-equal, exact origin, `crossOrigin` absent/false. 2. authData: rpIdHash = SHA-256(rp_id), UP and UV bits set, sign count rule (counter anomaly reported as Counter). 3. ES256 signature over authData || SHA-256(clientDataJSON) with p256. 4. Registration: attestation format "none" only; extract credential ID and key. 5. Tests use an in-test software authenticator (p256 key) to build valid ceremonies, then one-field mutations.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Order: type → challenge → origin → rpIdHash → flags → signature; no partial acceptance.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Origin prefix match instead of exact; UV not required.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p mc-webauthn-verify`; when W05-T008 fixtures exist, they verify identically (cross-implementation check). **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `assertion::valid_ceremony_verifies`; `assertion::wrong_type`; `assertion::wrong_challenge`; `assertion::origin_prefix_is_not_equal`; `assertion::cross_origin_true`; `assertion::wrong_rp_id_hash`; `assertion::up_missing`; `assertion::uv_missing`; `assertion::signature_bit_flip`; `assertion::counter_regression_flags_anomaly`; `assertion::malformed_auth_data`; `registration::valid_none_attestation`; `registration::non_none_attestation_rejected`; `registration::wrong_challenge`; `crosscheck::w05_fixtures_agree (#[ignore] until W05-T008 fixtures exist; then enabled)`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** One named test per WebAuthnError variant.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Valid ceremony verifies; every single-field mutation fails with the right variant.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Credential state, challenges, nonce consumption (callers).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Exact comparisons; check order; no lenient parsing.

### W01-T010-CUSTODY-MANIFEST-STORE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T010-CUSTODY-MANIFEST-STORE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write the byte-level storage for patches: a canonical list of changed files, and a store where every read re-checks the file's hash. This guarantees the exact bytes a human approved are the bytes that get published. **Precisely:** Implement the byte layer of `mc-custody`: the canonical patch manifest exactly as CONTRACT_AUTHORITY defines it, and a write-once tenant-scoped content-addressed object store (local filesystem backend; S3-compatible later) whose every read re-hashes the bytes.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-025: approved bytes must be exact; this layer has no dependency on contract types, so it runs early.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** CONTRACT_AUTHORITY patch manifest_digest format; 14 PatchCustodyV1; INV-11, INV-19.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09F.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** none (bytes).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `manifest::canonical(entries) -> Vec<u8>` (sorted raw path bytes; mode 100644/100755/120000/DELETE; SHA-256 or `-`; decimal size; LF); `store::ObjectStore::{put(tenant, bytes) -> digest, get(tenant, digest) -> Result<bytes, StoreError>}`; StoreError: NotFound, DigestMismatch, SizeMismatch, CrossTenant, AlreadyExistsDifferent. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct ManifestEntry { pub path: Vec<u8>, pub mode: EntryMode, pub sha256: Option<[u8; 32]>, pub size: u64 }`; `pub enum EntryMode { File100644, Exec100755, Symlink120000, Delete }`; `pub fn canonical(entries: &[ManifestEntry]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, ManifestError>`; `pub fn manifest_digest(bytes: &[u8]) -> [u8; 32]`; `pub struct ObjectStore { root: PathBuf }` with `put(&self, tenant: Uuid, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<[u8; 32], StoreError>` and `get(&self, tenant: Uuid, digest: &[u8; 32], expected_size: u64) -> Result<Vec<u8>, StoreError>`; `pub enum StoreError { NotFound, DigestMismatch, SizeMismatch, CrossTenant, AlreadyExistsDifferent, Io }`; `pub enum ManifestError { Unsorted, Duplicate, UnsupportedMode, UnsupportedPath }`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-custody/src/{manifest,store}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-custody/tests/{manifest,store}*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** CONTRACT_AUTHORITY.md.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T000 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-custody/src/manifest.rs`; `crates/mc-custody/src/store.rs`; `crates/mc-custody/tests/manifest.rs`; `crates/mc-custody/tests/store.rs`; `crates/mc-custody/tests/golden/*.manifest (hand-computed)`. **Steps:** 1. Manifest canonicalizer; reject unsupported path/object features (submodule, non-UTF-8 handling per spec). 2. Store keyed by (tenant, sha256); write-once (same bytes = idempotent; different bytes = error). 3. Every `get` recomputes digest and size; mismatch fails closed. 4. Golden tests with hand-computed manifests.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Bytes are never regenerated; a signed URL is transport only.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Unsorted manifest; read returning bytes without re-hash.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p mc-custody manifest store`. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `manifest::golden_add_modify`; `manifest::golden_delete`; `manifest::golden_mode_and_symlink`; `manifest::sorted_by_raw_bytes_not_utf8_order`; `manifest::duplicate_path_rejected`; `manifest::unsupported_mode_rejected`; `store::put_is_idempotent_same_bytes`; `store::put_different_bytes_same_key_rejected`; `store::get_rehashes_corrupted_file`; `store::get_wrong_size_rejected`; `store::cross_tenant_read_with_matching_hash_rejected`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Corrupted stored file → DigestMismatch; cross-tenant read with matching hash → CrossTenant.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Golden manifests byte-exact; every negative fails closed.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Pins, retention, snapshots (T021).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Manifest format byte-exact; hash on every read.

### W01-T011-API-FREEZE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T011-API-FREEZE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Write down (as Rust traits, no implementation) the exact functions every security component offers. After this, all remaining W01 tasks can be built in parallel because everyone codes against the same function names. **Precisely:** Freeze every W01 interface as Rust traits and types in `src/api.rs` of each crate (no implementations), finalizing the @v1 sketches in CROSS_WORKSTREAM_INTEGRATION_CONTRACT.md. After this, all implementation units and doubles run in parallel against fixed seams.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Without frozen seams, parallel units would invent incompatible calls.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 07, 29, 50, 51, 52, 53, 56, 14, 35; INV-03, INV-06, INV-10, INV-13, INV-17, INV-22.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** enabler for all W01 claims.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc-contracts` (I-01).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `api.rs` in: mc-lease (LeaseAuthority, LeaseRef, LeaseBoundOp, LeaseError — sketch I-03), mc-effects (EffectAuthority, EffectId, AuthorizationProof, SendClaim, UnknownReason, EffectError — sketch I-04), mc-journal (JournalSequencer: append(entry) → [JournalWitnessReceiptV1;2]; fence(JournalEpochFenceV1); read_range(epoch, from, to)), mc-ingestion (EvidenceIngest, IngestOutcome — sketch I-05), mc-gate-authority (GateAuthority: decide(evaluation_id), revoke(RevocationRecordV1)), mc-custody (CustodyStore: put_bundle, pin(publication_id), unpin, read(audience, artifact, operation) ; CustodyState enum), mc-approval-authority (ApprovalAuthority: prepare(plan_id), finish(challenge_id, raw assertion), consume_assertion(assertion_digest, publication_id)), mc-credential-lifecycle (CredentialRegistry: current_binding(credential_id), apply(action) ; operation enum INITIAL_BOOTSTRAP…CHANGE_BINDING), mc-resolution (ResolutionAuthority: open_case, propose_action, finish), mc-plan-verifier (PlanVerifier: verify(plan request) → VerifiedPublicationPlanV2), mc-publication-planner (Planner: submit(publication_id)), mc-git-gateway (Gateway: execute(effect_id) ; GatewayDenial enum), mc-negotiation (Negotiator: negotiate(advertised, policy) → pinned set digest), mc-bootstrap (EpochFence, RestoredAuthorityFilter: is_denied(entitlement)). Plus `CROSS_WORKSTREAM_INTEGRATION_CONTRACT`-style doc `docs/INTERFACES.md` listing each trait. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** All I/O traits are `#[async_trait] pub trait X: Send + Sync`; `Negotiator` and `RestoredAuthorityFilter` are deliberately synchronous pure traits (`pub trait X: Send + Sync`, no I/O); all wire records are mc-contracts types. **mc-lease:** `LeaseRef { tenant_id, job_id, attempt_id, owner: Uuid, lease_generation: u64, authority_epoch: Uuid }`; `LeaseRequest { tenant_id, runner_id: Uuid, capability: CapabilityProfileV1 }`; `LeaseOffer { lease: LeaseRef, binding: AttemptBindingV2, expires_at_us: i64 }`; `enum LeaseBoundOp { Heartbeat, Complete, ArtifactPromotion, VerificationSubmission, ModelAuthorization, DependencyGrant }`; `LiveLease { lease: LeaseRef, sampled_at_us: i64 }`; `enum LeaseError { Expired, WrongOwner, StaleGeneration, WrongEpoch, IllegalState, ClockUnhealthy, Conflict, NoWork }`; `trait LeaseAuthority { async fn acquire(&self, r: LeaseRequest) -> Result<LeaseOffer, LeaseError>; async fn heartbeat(&self, l: &LeaseRef) -> Result<i64, LeaseError>; async fn complete(&self, l: &LeaseRef, result_digest: [u8; 32]) -> Result<bool /* late */, LeaseError>; async fn authorize_lease_bound(&self, conn: &mut PgConnection, l: &LeaseRef, op: LeaseBoundOp) -> Result<LiveLease, LeaseError>; }` — one transaction-compatible signature for real and fake (audit F-8): the caller passes `&mut *tx` of **its own** transaction, so the real predicate is atomic with the caller's mutation and the fake receives the same connection; no associated `Tx` type; `pub use sqlx::PgConnection;`. All consumers' tables and `mc_authority` live in **one PostgreSQL database** (separate schemas, doc 07 modular monolith), which is what makes the shared transaction possible. **mc-effects:** `struct EffectId(pub Uuid)`; `struct AuthorizationProof { kind: AuthorizationKind, lease: Option<LeaseRef>, approval_assertion_digest: Option<[u8; 32]>, capability_id: Option<Uuid> }`; `enum AuthorizationKind { LeaseBound, PublicationCapability, CheckProjection }`; `struct SendClaim { effect_id: EffectId, dispatcher_session: Uuid, certificate_digest: [u8; 32] }`; `enum UnknownReason { RequestDeadline, ResponseLost, DispatcherCrashed, ProviderAmbiguous }`; `enum DurabilityFailure { AckLost(u8), Partition(u8), ChainRejected, EpochFenced }`; `enum EffectError { DoNotSend(DurabilityFailure), AuthorityRevoked, DeadlineExceeded, NotPending, AlreadyArmed, Conflict, Fenced }`; `trait EffectAuthority { async fn create_pending(&self, i: EffectIntentV2) -> Result<EffectId, EffectError>; ### W01-T012-WIRE-CANONICAL
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T012-WIRE-CANONICAL
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the real codec: turn each of the 85 record types into canonical bytes, compute their hashes and check signatures, matching the architecture's test vectors exactly. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-wire` (I-02@v1): the `Canonical` trait for all 85 contracts on top of `mc-cbor`, domain-separated digests, the Ed25519 signature input, and the `mc-wire-vectors` CLI for W06's parity harness.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** All signed or hashed records depend on byte-exact encoding (ADR-005/024); P0-06A/B need this independent Rust implementation.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** ENCODING.md in full: `[type_name,1,fields…]`; enums as zero-based codes; options `[]`/`[x]`; sets sorted by encoded bytes and unique; `D(T,x)=SHA-256("MASTERCODER/V2.1/"+T+"/sha256"||0x00||CBOR(x))`; signature input `"MASTERCODER/V2.1/signature"||0x00||CBOR([contract_type,payload_digest,signer_uuid,key_uuid,credential_generation])`, pure Ed25519.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-06A, P0-06B (W06), P0-06C.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc-contracts`, `mc-cbor`; wire-vectors.json, signature-vectors.json.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** crate `mc-wire` (Canonical impls, `signature_input`, `verify_detached`); binary `mc-wire-vectors` emitting `vector-results.json` exactly per the W06-T003 schema (I-13). **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub trait Canonical: Sized { const TYPE_NAME: &'static str; const WIRE_REVISION: u64 = 1; fn to_cbor(&self) -> Vec<u8>; fn from_cbor(b: &[u8]) -> Result<Self, WireError>; fn digest(&self) -> [u8; 32]; }` implemented for all 85 contracts; `pub fn digest_of(type_name: &str, cbor: &[u8]) -> [u8; 32]`; `pub fn signature_input(contract_type: &str, payload_digest: &[u8; 32], signer: Uuid, key: Uuid, credential_generation: u64) -> Vec<u8>`; `pub fn verify_detached<T: Canonical>(record: &T, sig: &[u8; 64], pubkey: &[u8; 32], signer: Uuid, key: Uuid, generation: u64) -> Result<(), WireError>`; `pub enum WireError { Cbor(CborError), UnknownType, WrongRevision, FieldCount, Enum, Width, Lexical, SetOrder, Duplicate, Option, Signature }`; CLI `mc-wire-vectors --vectors <path> --out <path>`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-wire/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-wire/tests/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-contracts`, `mc-cbor`, vectors. **Independence rule:** never open `ws05-web-approval-ux/packages/wire-ts/**`; `check_spec.py` is not an oracle.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T004 INTEGRATED; W01-T005 INTEGRATED; W06-T003 INTEGRATED (vector-results schema).
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-wire/src/lib.rs (owned)`; `crates/mc-wire/src/{canonical.rs,encode.rs,decode.rs,digest.rs,signature.rs,error.rs}`; `crates/mc-wire/src/bin/mc-wire-vectors.rs`; `crates/mc-wire/tests/{accept.rs,reject.rs,signatures.rs,properties.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Generate or hand-write the Canonical impls by walking FIELD_ORDER; the impl is driven by `mc-contracts` metadata, not by per-type hand code. 2. Decoder maps CborError to WireError and adds UnknownType, WrongRevision, FieldCount, Enum, Width, Lexical, SetOrder, Duplicate, Option. 3. Digest and signature-input functions; verify with ed25519-dalek, always recomputing the digest. 4. Property: env vars (locale/TZ) do not change bytes.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-18; no normalization of malformed signed input; signature verification never skips digest recomputation.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Set re-sorted silently on decode; trailing bytes ignored; enum code drift.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p mc-wire`: 85 ACCEPT byte+digest equality; 355 REJECT reject; 6/6 signatures verify and 6 corrupted reject. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `accept::all_85_bytes_equal`; `accept::all_85_sha256_equal`; `reject::all_355_reject`; `reject::duplicate_set_element`; `reject::two_element_option`; `reject::unknown_enum_code`; `reject::wrong_revision`; `signatures::six_valid_verify`; `signatures::six_corrupted_reject`; `properties::decode_encode_identity_per_type`; `properties::locale_and_tz_do_not_change_bytes`; `mc-wire-vectors` output identical on two runs. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** All 355 REJECT vectors plus duplicate set element, 2-element option, unknown enum code, wrong revision.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** 85/85, 355/355, 6/6 + 6/6; CLI output stable across runs.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** `mc-wire-vectors` JSON SHA-256; test counts. A1 sends INTERFACE_NOTICE I-02@v1.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** TypeScript codec (W05); parity harness (W06).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Domain strings byte-exact; independence rule respected.

### W01-T013-P0-10A-HARNESS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T013-P0-10A-HARNESS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the test program ("harness") that will later prove claim P0-10A: "PostgreSQL tenant row and composite-key isolation". It runs one normal case and then 5 attack/failure cases, each from a clean start, and writes the evidence files. In this task it only runs locally against fakes (DRY_RUN); the real run is the separate RUN task after G2/G3. **Precisely:** Build the P0-10A harness on the role framework: two tenants with identical local IDs; every role × operation × tenant combination; the claim's injections; machine-readable results.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** P0-10A proves tenant isolation and writer separation structurally.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 07 grants and tenant isolation; 18; ADR-013/019; INV-15.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** P0-10A (primary).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** ScopeV1; role framework (T007).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `claims/P0-10A/harness` binary emitting `RLS-query-matrix.json`, `role-grants.sql.txt`, `denial-results.json`; `claims/P0-10A/run-manifest.draft.json`. **Evidence files the harness writes:** `RLS-query-matrix.json`, `role-grants.sql.txt`, `denial-results.json`, `P0-10A/run-manifest.json`, `P0-10A/fixture-digests.json`, `P0-10A/evidence-manifest.sha256`, `P0-10A/independent-review.json` (run-manifest/evidence-manifest/independent-review are finalized only in the RUN task).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-10A/**` (except `harness/Cargo.toml`)
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-authority-db`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T007 INTEGRATED; W01-T008 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `claims/P0-10A/harness/src/main.rs`; `claims/P0-10A/harness/src/{scenarios.rs,report.rs}`; `claims/P0-10A/fixtures/** (synthetic only)`; `claims/P0-10A/fixture-digests.json`; `claims/P0-10A/run-manifest.draft.json`; `claims/P0-10A/README.md (how to run DRY_RUN; which injections; expected outputs)`. **Steps:** 1. Seed two tenants with identical local IDs. 2. For each runtime role: select/insert/update/delete/join on every base table, own and foreign tenant. 3. Injections: cross-tenant FK, unset GUC, falsified GUC, reused pooled connection, any SECURITY DEFINER path. 4. Emit results with expected vs observed per case. **Claim procedure the harness must automate (verbatim from atomic-prototype-claims.json P0-10A):** (1) Provision exactly the sealed cell and empty evidence directory named P0-10A. (2) Run select/insert/update/delete and join via API/worker/readonly roles; try cross-tenant references. (3) Run each semicolon-separated injected case separately from reset baseline; retain both control and failure traces. (4) Evaluate the declared observations and thresholds; reviewer signs evidence manifest and declares PASS, FAIL or INCONCLUSIVE.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-15.
- **FAILURE CASES:** A positive control failing (harness wrong) reported as PASS.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** DRY_RUN of the full matrix; output identical on rerun. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `scenarios::positive_control`; `scenarios::injection_a_unset_session_tenant`; `scenarios::injection_b_falsify_session_tenant`; `scenarios::injection_c_reused_pooled_connection`; `scenarios::injection_d_security_definer_path`; `scenarios::injection_e_cross_tenant_fk`; `report::rerun_is_byte_identical`; `report::every_case_has_expected_and_observed`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** All P0-10A injections. **Claim injections — each one a separate scenario from a reset baseline, keeping control and failure traces:** (a) Unset session tenant; (b) falsify session tenant; (c) reused pooled connection; (d) SECURITY DEFINER path; (e) cross-tenant FK.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Zero cross-tenant success; all same-tenant positive controls succeed. **Claim expected observations:** No cross-tenant row or reference succeeds; authorized same-tenant controls succeed. **Threshold:** Zero unauthorized acceptances, effects or disclosure across all listed cases; every legitimate control succeeds. **DRY_RUN must show:** positive control succeeds and every injection produces the expected observation.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** DRY_RUN outputs SHA-256 (not claim evidence).
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Claim execution (W01-RUN-P0-10A).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Matrix completeness; positive controls present.

### W01-T014-LEASE-CORE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T014-LEASE-CORE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the lease system: a runner "borrows" a job for a limited time, and once the time has run out by the database clock, every action it tries is refused, even if nothing has cleaned it up yet. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-lease` (I-03 real): lease acquisition, heartbeat, completion, the lease-bound authorization predicate with DB-clock expiry sampled after locks, the reaper (records only), and the clock-health guard.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-022: an unreaped expired lease must not keep authority.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 07 "Lease transaction and actual expiry"; 19 Job/JobAttempt; 29 lock order and predicate; INV-13; equality is expired; heartbeat cannot renew an expired lease; G-043 clock health.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-08A.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** AttemptBindingV2, AttemptEventV2, CapabilityProfileV1; `mc-lease::api` (T011).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `LeaseAuthority` implementation; migrations `db/authority/lease/*`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct PgLeaseAuthority { pool: PgPool, cfg: LeaseConfig }` implementing `LeaseAuthority` (the predicate runs on the caller's `&mut PgConnection`, never on a connection it opens itself); `pub struct LeaseConfig { pub lease_duration_ms: u64, pub clock_rollback_tolerance_ms: u64 }` loaded from env/config, never constants; `pub async fn reap_once(pool: &PgPool) -> Result<u64, LeaseError>` (records expiry only).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-lease/src/**` (except `api.rs`), `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-lease/tests/**`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/lease/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-authority-db`, `mc-wire`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T011 INTEGRATED; W01-T012 INTEGRATED; W01-T007 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-lease/src/lib.rs (add modules)`; `crates/mc-lease/src/{authority.rs,predicate.rs,reaper.rs,clock_health.rs,config.rs}`; `db/authority/lease/0001_jobs_attempts.sql`; `db/authority/lease/0002_grants.sql`; `crates/mc-lease/tests/{boundary.rs,lock_wait.rs,reaper.rs,clock_health.rs,negative.rs,grep_now.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Acquire: `SELECT … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` READY work matching exact capability; generation + 1; expiry from `clock_timestamp()`; immutable attempt binding. 2. Every lease-bound op: lock job/attempt rows in doc 29 order, **then** sample `clock_timestamp()`, then one predicate on owner, generation, epoch, allowed state, `lease_expires_at > sample`. 3. Heartbeat writes a new expiry only if the old one passed. 4. Reaper records expiry only; reassignment = new attempt + generation. 5. Late completion → quarantined audit fact; settlement call-out still allowed. 6. Clock-health: DB clock moving backward beyond the configured tolerance (config, not constant) → ClockUnhealthy until fenced recovery.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-13; `now()`/`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`/`statement_timestamp()` never used for acceptance; strict `>`.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Transaction-start time used; expired lease renewed; request that waited on a lock past T accepted.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Boundary T-1 ms / T / T+1 ms; lock-wait test (second session holds FOR UPDATE across T); reaper-disabled test; clock-rollback guard test. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `boundary::accept_at_t_minus_1ms`; `boundary::reject_at_t_equal`; `boundary::reject_at_t_plus_1ms`; `boundary::active_state_but_expired_rejects_every_op (all 6 LeaseBoundOp)`; `lock_wait::request_waiting_on_lock_past_expiry_rejects`; `reaper::reaper_disabled_still_rejects`; `reaper::reaper_only_records`; `reaper::reassignment_increments_generation`; `clock_health::rollback_beyond_tolerance_stops_authority`; `negative::wrong_owner`; `negative::stale_generation`; `negative::wrong_epoch`; `negative::heartbeat_cannot_renew_expired`; `negative::late_completion_quarantined_but_settlement_allowed`; `shared_tx::predicate_and_caller_insert_commit_or_roll_back_together`; `grep_now::no_now_in_acceptance_sql`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Wrong owner, generation, epoch each alone; equality case rejects.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** After DB expiry every new authoritative transition rejects although state is ACTIVE; `git grep -n "now()" db/authority/lease crates/mc-lease` finds no acceptance use.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report; the grep output.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** HTTP server (T029); harness (T039).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
