### W01-T015-LEASE-DOUBLE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T015-LEASE-DOUBLE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build an in-memory fake of the lease system with a controllable clock, so the W03, W04 and W06 lanes can test against leases before the real one is merged. **Precisely:** Publish `doubles/lease/v1` (I-03 double): an in-memory `LeaseAuthority` with a controllable clock and lock-wait injection, behaviourally matching the predicate of 07/29, for W03/W04/W06 and W01-T031.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Consumers build against the lease API before the real one is integrated.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 07/29 predicate; INV-13.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** none (enabler).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc-lease::api`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `lease_double::{FakeLease::new(clock), FakeClock::{set, advance}, inject_lock_wait(duration)}` implementing the **same** `LeaseAuthority` signature as the real one, including `authorize_lease_bound(&self, conn: &mut PgConnection, …)` (audit F-8): the fake receives the caller's transaction connection, decides from its own clock/state and never opens a second transaction; README labeled TEST_ONLY. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct FakeClock` with `set_us(i64)`, `advance_ms(u64)`, `now_us() -> i64`; `pub struct FakeLease::new(clock: FakeClock)` implementing the same `LeaseAuthority` signature (`authorize_lease_bound(&self, conn: &mut PgConnection, …)`; it receives the caller's connection and never opens another); `fn inject_lock_wait(&self, ms: u64)`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/doubles/lease/v1/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/doubles/lease/v1/tests/**`, `ws01-core-security/doubles/lease/v1/README.md`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-lease::api`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T011 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `doubles/lease/v1/src/lib.rs`; `doubles/lease/v1/src/{fake.rs,clock.rs}`; `doubles/lease/v1/tests/parity.rs`; `doubles/lease/v1/README.md`. **Steps:** 1. In-memory state per job/attempt. 2. Same error variants and same equality rule as the real predicate. 3. Lock-wait injection delays the clock sample to after the wait.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Labeled TEST_ONLY; never used by a claim RUN.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Double more permissive than the real predicate.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** `cargo test -p lease-double`: the same boundary cases as T014. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `parity::accept_before_expiry`; `parity::reject_at_equality`; `parity::reject_after_expiry`; `parity::lock_wait_past_expiry_rejects`; `parity::wrong_owner_generation_epoch_each_reject`; `parity::heartbeat_cannot_renew_expired`; `parity::called_inside_caller_transaction_on_real_testdb`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Equality case rejects; lock-wait past T rejects.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Boundary behaviour identical to the 07/29 rules.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report. A1 sends INTERFACE_NOTICE I-03 double v1.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Persistence.
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Never more permissive than the spec.

### W01-T016-EFFECTS-STORE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T016-EFFECTS-STORE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the database side of "external effects" (any call to GitHub, a package registry or a model provider): each call is recorded before it happens and can be sent at most once. **Precisely:** Implement the PostgreSQL side of `mc-effects`: immutable EffectIntentV2, append-only EffectStateV1 revisions with CAS current pointer, DbStartCertificateV1 issuance in the `start` transaction, and exactly one SEND_ARMED claim per effect.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-023: the DB side of the dispatch protocol; W02/W03/W04 send through it.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 50 immutable request and state records, dispatch steps; 18 effect constraints; 29 effect dispatch (5 s dispatch window, 60 s request deadline — values from config, matching doc 29); INV-13, INV-17.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09G/H, P0-08D, P0-03C/E, P0-10G/H.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** EffectIntentV2, EffectStateV1, DbStartCertificateV1; `mc-effects::api`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** store functions used by T030: `create_pending`, `start` (locks, authority recheck through the caller's AuthorizationProof, deadlines, CAS PENDING→STARTED, certificate), `arm` (single claim), `project_observed`, `mark_unknown`; migrations `db/authority/effects/*`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct EffectStore { pool: PgPool, cfg: EffectConfig }`; `pub struct EffectConfig { pub dispatch_window_ms: u64 /* 5000 per doc 29 */, pub request_deadline_ms: u64 /* 60000 per doc 29 */ }` from config; methods `create_pending`, `mark_prepared(id, receipts)`, `start(id, AuthorizationProof) -> DbStartCertificateV1`, `arm(id, session) -> SendClaim`, `project_observed(id, ProviderResultV1)`, `mark_unknown(id, UnknownReason)`, `current(id)`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-effects/src/store*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-effects/tests/store*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/effects/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-authority-db`, `mc-wire`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T011 INTEGRATED; W01-T012 INTEGRATED; W01-T007 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-effects/src/store.rs (and store/ submodules)`; `db/authority/effects/0001_intent_state.sql`; `db/authority/effects/0002_start_certificate_and_claim.sql`; `db/authority/effects/0003_grants.sql`; `crates/mc-effects/tests/store_*.rs`. **Steps:** 1. effect_intent unique (tenant, logical_operation_id, effect_kind, call_attempt), immutable. 2. effect_state revisions with prior-digest chain; CAS pointer. 3. Unique partial index guaranteeing one SEND_ARMED per effect_id. 4. Deadlines read from config.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** UNKNOWN never becomes absence; no second claim.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Second SEND_ARMED under concurrency; state rewrite instead of append.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** 1,000-iteration concurrent `arm` → exactly one claim; CAS conflict test. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `store_intent::intent_is_immutable_update_denied`; `store_intent::duplicate_logical_key_rejected`; `store_state::append_only_with_prior_digest_chain`; `store_state::cas_conflict_returns_conflict`; `store_claim::concurrent_arm_1000_iterations_exactly_one_claim`; `store_claim::arm_after_dispatch_window_denied`; `store_claim::second_arm_already_armed`; `store_unknown::unknown_never_becomes_absent`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** UPDATE/DELETE on intent denied; arm after dispatch deadline denied.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** One claim per effect under contention; append-only history.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report with race statistics.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Journal (T017); orchestration and crash matrix (T030).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Uniqueness constraints; append-only.

### W01-T017-JOURNAL-WITNESS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T017-JOURNAL-WITNESS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the journal: every external call is written to two independent stores, each of which signs a receipt, before the call may be sent. This is what allows recovery after a crash or a database restore. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-journal`: a sequencer writing hash-chained ExternalEffectJournalV1 entries to **two independent witness processes**, each with its own storage directory, fsync and signing key, each returning a signed JournalWitnessReceiptV1; both receipts required; JournalEpochFenceV1 handling.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-023 (findings 9–11): witness durability precedes send; restore must recover intents lost from the DB (G-051 two storage domains).
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 50 journal durability and writer topology; restore section; INV-01, INV-17.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09G/H, P0-08D, P0-10G/H/I.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** ExternalEffectJournalV1, JournalWitnessReceiptV1, JournalEpochFenceV1; `mc-journal::api`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `JournalSequencer` implementation; witness binary `mc-journal-witness` (one process per copy); storage directories from config. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct WitnessConfig { pub storage_dir: PathBuf, pub signing_key_path: PathBuf, pub listen: SocketAddr }`; `pub struct Sequencer::new(witnesses: [WitnessClient; 2])` implementing `JournalSequencer`; witness binary `mc-journal-witness --config <file>`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-journal/src/**` (except `api.rs`), `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-journal/tests/**`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/journal/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-wire`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T011 INTEGRATED; W01-T012 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-journal/src/lib.rs (add modules)`; `crates/mc-journal/src/{sequencer.rs,witness.rs,storage.rs,chain.rs,receipt.rs,config.rs}`; `crates/mc-journal/src/bin/mc-journal-witness.rs`; `db/authority/journal/0001_high_water.sql`; `crates/mc-journal/tests/{two_copy.rs,chain.rs,epoch.rs,restore_read.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Entries carry complete intent bytes and reconstruction metadata (no digest-only pointers into the DB). 2. Witness: verify chain continuity, reject gaps, conflicting claims and fenced epochs; fsync before signing. 3. Sequencer returns only when both signed receipts arrived. 4. `read_range` for restore enumeration.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Single-copy ack is never durable; old epoch appends rejected.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Receipt signed before fsync; chain gap accepted.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Two witnesses in temp dirs; kill one → append fails (DoNotSend); restart → continues. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `two_copy::append_returns_two_receipts`; `two_copy::one_witness_down_append_fails_do_not_send`; `two_copy::restart_continues_chain`; `two_copy::receipt_signed_only_after_fsync (test hook)`; `chain::gap_rejected`; `chain::conflicting_second_send_armed_rejected`; `chain::tampered_receipt_detected`; `epoch::old_epoch_append_rejected_after_fence`; `restore_read::read_range_returns_complete_intent_bytes`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Chain gap, conflicting second SEND_ARMED entry, old-epoch append, tampered receipt — each rejected.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** No success without two receipts in any injected case.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Cloud storage domains (W06 infra for RUN).
- **BLOCKERS:** none for BUILD (two local directories).
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** fsync-before-sign; chain checks.

- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build an in-memory fake of the effects + journal system with switches to simulate crashes and lost acknowledgements, so other people can test their GitHub/model/fetch calls early. **Precisely:** Publish `doubles/effects-journal/v1` (I-04 double): in-memory EffectAuthority with a two-copy witness and fault hooks (ack loss per copy, partition, copy loss, crash at each of the 6 crash-matrix boundaries of doc 50).

### W01-T019-EVIDENCE-INGESTION
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T019-EVIDENCE-INGESTION
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the front door for scan evidence: it checks who signed a scan result, whether they were allowed to, and whether their lease was still valid, before storing it. A hacked ordinary server cannot plant fake results. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-ingestion` (EvidenceIngest, I-05 part 1): verify each signed submission (detached signature, producer key role, current trust membership and generation, tenant/repository epoch, attempt binding, lease predicate via I-03, manifest/unit membership) before inserting immutable receipts — completion and evidence receipts through **evidence_ingest_writer**, verification receipts through **verification_ingest_writer** (07 per-input writer matrix; audit F-20). The two writers use separate constrained connection pools; neither role can insert the other's table.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-019: a compromised ordinary API must not manufacture gate inputs.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 07 matrix; 06 gate derivation; CONTRACT_AUTHORITY producer table; INV-03, INV-06.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-07B.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** AnalysisCompletionReceiptV1, EvidenceReceiptV2, VerificationReceiptV2, ProducerEventV2, SignedEnvelopeV1, TrustBundleV1, TrustMemberV1, ExpectedAnalysisManifestV1.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** EvidenceIngest implementation; migrations `db/authority/ingestion/*` (quarantine table, replay index). **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct PgIngest<L: LeaseAuthority> { evidence_pool: PgPool /* evidence_ingest_writer */, verification_pool: PgPool /* verification_ingest_writer */, lease: L, trust: TrustView }` implementing `EvidenceIngest` (completion/evidence → evidence_pool, verification → verification_pool; audit F-20); verification order fixed in `verify.rs`: signature → key role → trust membership → trust generation → epoch → attempt binding → lease → manifest membership.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-ingestion/src/**` (except `api.rs`), `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-ingestion/tests/**`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/ingestion/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-wire`, `mc-authority-db`, `mc-lease::api` + `doubles/lease/v1` (tests), `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T011 INTEGRATED; W01-T012 INTEGRATED; W01-T007 INTEGRATED; W01-T014 INTEGRATED; W01-T015 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-ingestion/src/lib.rs (add modules)`; `crates/mc-ingestion/src/{verify.rs,ingest.rs,quarantine.rs}`; `db/authority/ingestion/0001_quarantine_replay.sql`; `db/authority/ingestion/0002_grants.sql`; `crates/mc-ingestion/tests/{positive.rs,negative.rs,replay.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Verification order: signature → key role → trust membership/generation → epoch → attempt binding → lease predicate → manifest membership. 2. Route by validated submission kind: completion/evidence → evidence_ingest_writer pool; verification → verification_ingest_writer pool; the lease predicate runs on the same connection/transaction as that insert (`authorize_lease_bound(&mut *tx, …)`). 3. Insert only after all pass. 4. Duplicate equal digest → Replay; unequal → Quarantined. 5. One generic test builds `PgIngest` with the real `PgLeaseAuthority` and with `FakeLease`, both inside one real TestDb transaction (audit F-8).
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Signature ≠ authority; DB row shape is never trusted.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Accepting a valid signature from a key with the wrong producer role.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Positive path for each of the three submission kinds. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `positive::completion_accepted`; `positive::evidence_accepted`; `positive::verification_accepted`; `negative::bad_signature`; `negative::wrong_producer_role_valid_signature`; `negative::stale_trust_generation`; `negative::old_epoch`; `negative::attempt_binding_mismatch`; `negative::expired_lease_via_double`; `negative::foreign_manifest_unit`; `replay::equal_duplicate_is_replay`; `replay::conflicting_duplicate_quarantined`; `negative::ordinary_api_role_cannot_insert_receipts`; `writers::evidence_writer_cannot_insert_verification_receipt`; `writers::verification_writer_cannot_insert_completion_or_evidence`; `writers::valid_verification_signature_via_evidence_writer_denied`; `generic::same_consumer_with_real_and_fake_lease_in_one_tx`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Wrong producer role; stale trust generation; old epoch; expired lease (via double); foreign manifest unit; duplicate conflicting receipt; evidence_ingest_writer cannot insert a verification receipt; verification_ingest_writer cannot insert a completion or evidence receipt; a validly signed verification receipt routed through the evidence writer is denied.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Every invalid provenance rejected; equal duplicate = Replay.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Gate commit (T031).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Verification order; insert after all checks.

### W01-T021-CUSTODY-PINS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T021-CUSTODY-PINS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the rest of patch custody: pins that keep approved patch bytes alive while publication is in progress or uncertain, retention rules, legal hold and safe deletion. **Precisely:** Complete `mc-custody` (I-16): PatchCustodyV1 bundles and snapshots over the T010 store, authenticated reads (tenant, audience, artifact, operation, expiry), pins by publication ID, retention through latest expiry + 7 days, extension while any effect is STARTED/UNKNOWN/MANUAL_RECONCILIATION, legal hold, DELETION_PENDING, LOSS_OF_RECONCILIATION_EVIDENCE.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-025 (finding 13): exact bytes survive display, approval, publication and reconciliation.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 14 PatchCustodyV1; 24 custody exception; ChangedObjectV1, ArtifactCustodyEntryV1, PatchCandidateV2; INV-11, INV-16, INV-19.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09F, P0-10C.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** PatchCustodyV1, PatchCandidateV2, ChangedObjectV1, ArtifactCustodyEntryV1; `mc-custody::api`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** CustodyStore implementation; migrations `db/authority/custody/*`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct PgCustody { pool: PgPool, store: ObjectStore, effects: Arc<dyn EffectAuthority>, cfg: CustodyConfig }` implementing `CustodyStore`; `pub struct CustodyConfig { pub retention_after_expiry_days: u32 /* 7 per doc 14 */ }`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-custody/src/{pins,snapshot,reader,retention,service}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-custody/tests/{pins,snapshot,reader,retention}*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/custody/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** T010 manifest/store modules, `mc-wire`, `mc-authority-db`, `mc-effects::api`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T010 INTEGRATED; W01-T011 INTEGRATED; W01-T012 INTEGRATED; W01-T007 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-custody/src/{pins.rs,snapshot.rs,reader.rs,retention.rs,service.rs}`; `db/authority/custody/0001_custody.sql`; `db/authority/custody/0002_pins_holds.sql`; `db/authority/custody/0003_grants.sql`; `crates/mc-custody/tests/{pins.rs,snapshot.rs,reader.rs,retention.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Custody state machine and pins. 2. Retention computed from the latest expiry + 7 days (duration from config matching doc 14). 3. Deletion request → revoke unpublished plan/challenges/assertion; DELETION_PENDING during uncertainty. 4. Reads check audience/operation/expiry then re-hash.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Ordinary API cannot replace bytes or change pins.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Releasing a pin while an effect is UNKNOWN.

Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Unpin with an UNKNOWN effect; read with wrong audience; read after expiry.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Pinned bytes survive all phases; deletion only after allowed terminal reconciliation.

### W01-T023-VERSION-NEGOTIATION
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T023-VERSION-NEGOTIATION
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build version negotiation: the runner and server agree on exactly which record formats they speak before any work is handed out, with no silent downgrade. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-negotiation`: authenticated exact `(type, wire_revision, registry_digest)` negotiation, pinning the chosen set digest into the session and capability digest, rejecting any later message whose type/revision/registry differs.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** 20/47/ENCODING require exact negotiation with no downgrade or mid-attempt change.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 47 (no min_reader_minor, no bitmap); ENCODING version and extension rule; CapabilityProfileV1.protocol_registry_digest; INV-18.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-06D.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** CapabilityProfileV1; `mc-negotiation::api`; I-09 spec (T003).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** Negotiator implementation. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct ExactNegotiator` implementing `Negotiator`; pinned digest = SHA-256 of the canonical CBOR of the sorted tuple array (domain string `MASTERCODER/V2.1/` per ENCODING.md).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-negotiation/src/**` (except `api.rs`), `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-negotiation/tests/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-wire`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T012 INTEGRATED; W01-T003 INTEGRATED; W01-T011 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-negotiation/src/lib.rs (add modules)`; `crates/mc-negotiation/src/negotiator.rs`; `crates/mc-negotiation/tests/{matrix.rs,negative.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Advertised set = sorted tuples in an authenticated session-open message. 2. Intersect with policy minimum; the full set needed for the attempt is required, else fail. 3. Pin the set digest; validator rejects any message with a different tuple.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-18; negotiation before authority issuance; no opaque extension forwarding.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Reduced set accepted silently; mid-session change allowed.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Reader/writer matrix: current, prior permitted minor (synthetic second registry digest with an identical type set), unsupported major. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `matrix::current_reader_accepted`; `matrix::prior_permitted_minor_with_identical_type_set`; `matrix::unsupported_major_rejected`; `matrix::pinned_digest_stable_across_messages`; `negative::downgrade_advertisement`; `negative::unknown_field`; `negative::mid_attempt_change`; `negative::newer_writer_incapable_reader`; `negative::unauthenticated_advertisement`; `negative::registry_digest_mismatch_identical_types`; `negative::unsorted_advertisement`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Downgrade advertisement; unknown field; mid-attempt change; newer writer to incapable reader; unauthenticated advertisement; registry digest mismatch with identical type list.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Only mutually supported authenticated sets proceed.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** HTTP (T029); harness (T038).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** No fallback path.

### W01-T024-P0-06C-HARNESS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T024-P0-06C-HARNESS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the test program ("harness") that will later prove claim P0-06C: "Attempt and tenant domain separation". It runs one normal case and then 3 attack/failure cases, each from a clean start, and writes the evidence files. In this task it only runs locally against fakes (DRY_RUN); the real run is the separate RUN task after G2/G3. **Precisely:** Build the P0-06C harness: binding-mutation fixtures differing in exactly one of tenant, repository, epoch, generation or record domain; prove every signature or digest reuse across records/tenants fails verification.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** P0-06C: changing any binding invalidates verification; an attempt signature cannot be replayed as an approval or effect signature.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** ENCODING digest/signature domains; SignedEnvelopeV1; ScopeV1; CONTRACT_AUTHORITY; INV-02, INV-15.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** P0-06C (primary).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc-wire`, `mc-contracts`, signature-vectors.json (public test seed only).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `claims/P0-06C/harness` emitting `binding-mutation-vectors.json`, `signature-verification-results.json`; `run-manifest.draft.json`. **Evidence files the harness writes:** `binding-mutation-vectors.json`, `signature-verification-results.json`, `P0-06C/run-manifest.json`, `P0-06C/fixture-digests.json`, `P0-06C/evidence-manifest.sha256`, `P0-06C/independent-review.json` (run-manifest/evidence-manifest/independent-review are finalized only in the RUN task).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/**` (except `harness/Cargo.toml`)
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-wire`, `mc-contracts`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T012 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `claims/P0-06C/harness/src/main.rs`; `claims/P0-06C/harness/src/{scenarios.rs,report.rs}`; `claims/P0-06C/fixtures/** (synthetic only)`; `claims/P0-06C/fixture-digests.json`; `claims/P0-06C/run-manifest.draft.json`; `claims/P0-06C/README.md (how to run DRY_RUN; which injections; expected outputs)`. **Steps:** 1. For AttemptBindingV2, HumanApprovalAssertionV2, EffectIntentV2, GitPublicationCapabilityV2 and ScopeV1-bearing records: baseline + single-field mutations (tenant_id, repository_id, repository_tenant_epoch, authority_epoch, lease_generation/credential_generation, contract_type domain). 2. Sign baselines with a staging test key; verify mutations against the baseline signature → REJECT. 3. Cross-record replay → REJECT. 4. Swap equal-width fields → digest change and REJECT. 5. Emit expected vs observed. **Claim procedure the harness must automate (verbatim from atomic-prototype-claims.json P0-06C):** (1) Provision exactly the sealed cell and empty evidence directory named P0-06C. (2) Generate signatures for baseline and attempt replay across each single-field mutation. (3) Run each semicolon-separated injected case separately from reset baseline; retain both control and failure traces. (4) Evaluate the declared observations and thresholds; reviewer signs evidence manifest and declares PASS, FAIL or INCONCLUSIVE.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-02, INV-15; signer role binding checked, not only math.
- **FAILURE CASES:** A mutation leaving the digest unchanged.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Every mutation class per record type; positive controls. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `scenarios::positive_control`; `scenarios::injection_a_reuse_attempt_signature_as_approval_or_effect`; `scenarios::injection_b_swap_equal_width_integer_positions`; `scenarios::injection_c_swap_equal_width_uuid_positions`; `report::rerun_is_byte_identical`; `report::every_case_has_expected_and_observed`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** All mutations and replays above. **Claim injections — each one a separate scenario from a reset baseline, keeping control and failure traces:** (a) Reuse attempt signature as approval or effect; (b) swap equal-width integer positions; (c) swap equal-width UUID positions.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** 100% positive controls verify; 100% mutations/replays reject; byte-identical rerun. **Claim expected observations:** Every changed binding/domain invalidates verification; no cross-record or cross-tenant reuse. **Threshold:** Zero unauthorized acceptances, effects or disclosure across all listed cases; every legitimate control succeeds. **DRY_RUN must show:** positive control succeeds and every injection produces the expected observation.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** DRY_RUN results SHA-256.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Claim execution.
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Mutation set covers every bound field.

### W01-T027-RESOLUTION-CORE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T027-RESOLUTION-CORE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the core of the Resolution Authority: open a case, propose an action, confirm it with a fresh fingerprint/security-key check, and record the decision. It can never produce a "pass" verdict. **Precisely:** Implement the core of `mc-resolution` (I-08 backend part 1): idempotent case open, ManualCaseStateV1 snapshots with CAS revision, ManualResolutionActionV1 → ResolutionChallengeV1 (5 min, one-use) → fresh UV ceremony (via `mc-webauthn-verify`) → signed ManualResolutionV1 committed by resolution_writer with expiry checked after locks; HTTP routes per T002.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-027 / INV-22: manual decisions never mint PASS.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 56 transaction rules; CONTRACT_AUTHORITY prior_state_digest; INV-22.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-10J.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** ManualCaseStateV1, ManualResolutionActionV1, ResolutionChallengeV1, ManualResolutionV1; I-08 spec (T002).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** ResolutionAuthority (core); routes; migrations `db/authority/resolution/*`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct PgResolution { pool, key: StagingSigningKey, cfg: ResolutionConfig }` implementing `ResolutionAuthority`; result type has no PASS variant; axum routes exactly per I-08 OpenAPI; `commit.rs` defines `pub trait FlowHandler: Send + Sync { fn handles(&self) -> &'static [(ResolutionKind, ResolutionDecision)] /* registry ManualResolutionActionV1.kind/.decision enums */; async fn handle(&self, case: &ManualCaseStateV1, action: &ManualResolutionActionV1) -> Result<FlowOutcome, ResolutionError>; }` and a registry; the registry is keyed by (kind, decision); with no handler registered for a pair, commit returns `NotAuthorized` (fail-closed until W01-T035 registers the seven handlers via `flows::register_all`). Pair table: `ManualResolutionActionV1` (kind, decision) per doc 56 flow, registry names exactly: untrusted agent oracle = (UNTRUSTED_ORACLE, PROVIDE_ORACLE); disputed finding = (DISPUTED_FINDING, REQUEST_FRESH_EVALUATION | REMAIN_BLOCKED); false-positive claim = (FALSE_POSITIVE, CREATE_POLICY_EXCLUSION); unsupported verification/C3 = (UNSUPPORTED_VERIFICATION, REQUEST_FRESH_EVALUATION | ACCEPT_RISK | REMAIN_BLOCKED); UNKNOWN effect with exact positive result = (EXTERNAL_EFFECT_UNCERTAINTY, CONFIRM_EFFECT); claimed nonexecution = (EXTERNAL_EFFECT_UNCERTAINTY, PROVE_TERMINAL_NONEXECUTION); uncertainty cannot be proved = (EXTERNAL_EFFECT_UNCERTAINTY, CLOSE_UNRESOLVED). APPROVAL_RECOVERY is not a doc-56 flow (credential recovery is W01-T036): the resolution authority rejects it fail-closed with NotAuthorized. REVOKE is accepted only where a flow above lists it (none in V2.1) — otherwise NotAuthorized.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-resolution/src/{case,action,challenge,commit,http}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-resolution/tests/core*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/resolution/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-webauthn-verify`, `mc-wire`, `mc-authority-db`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T011 INTEGRATED; W01-T012 INTEGRATED; W01-T007 INTEGRATED; W01-T009 INTEGRATED; W01-T002 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-resolution/src/{case.rs,action.rs,challenge.rs,commit.rs,http.rs}`; `db/authority/resolution/0001_cases.sql`; `db/authority/resolution/0002_actions_challenges.sql`; `db/authority/resolution/0003_grants.sql`; `crates/mc-resolution/tests/core.rs`. **Steps:** 1. Case open keyed by (tenant, subject, kind, idempotency key). 2. Lock order then `expires_at > clock_timestamp()` after locks; nonce consumption; audit/outbox atomically. 3. No code path writes a PASS verdict (enforced by type: the result enum has no PASS).
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-22; ordinary account recovery grants no resolution scope.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Expired challenge accepted after a lock wait; decision against a stale revision.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Lock-wait expiry test; idempotent open test. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `core::open_case_idempotent`; `core::stale_revision_rejected`; `core::challenge_reused_rejected`; `core::challenge_expired_after_lock_wait_rejected`; `core::publication_purpose_assertion_rejected`; `core::commit_writes_audit_and_outbox_atomically`; `core::ordinary_api_role_cannot_commit`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Stale revision; reused challenge; expired challenge; publication-purpose assertion used here.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Only scoped, proof-backed transitions; no PASS representable.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** The seven flows and separation rules (T035).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Clock after locks; no PASS type.

### W01-T028-CREDENTIAL-ENROLLMENT
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T028-CREDENTIAL-ENROLLMENT
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build how a person's security key gets registered as an approver, step by step in a fixed order, so nobody can register a key by skipping steps. **Precisely:** Implement the enrollment chain of `mc-credential-lifecycle`: ApprovalEnrollmentIntentV1 → ApprovalEnrollmentChallengeV1 → WebAuthnRegistrationCeremonyV1 → ApprovalEnrollmentActionV1 → ApprovalLifecycleChallengeV1 → ApprovalLifecycleConsentV1 / offline signatures → ApprovalEnrollmentAuthorizationV1 → ApprovalCredentialBindingV1, with the credential registry tables (approval_writer only).
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Finding 4 / SEC-01: the ordered chain closes the enrollment hole.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 52 "Enrollment wire binding"; ApprovalRecoveryPolicyV1; INV-10.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09B.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** all Approval* lifecycle contracts, WebAuthnRegistrationCeremonyV1, AuthorityScopeV1, SignedEnvelopeV1.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** CredentialRegistry (chain + current_binding); migrations `db/authority/credential/*`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct PgCredentials { pool, cfg: LifecycleConfig }` implementing `CredentialRegistry`; `apply` runs the chain and calls `operations::authorize(op, profile, …)` — W01-T028 writes `operations.rs`'s entry function as allowing only INITIAL_REGISTRATION and rejecting every other operation with `LifecycleError::OutOfOrder` (fail-closed); W01-T036 replaces that file with the full matrix.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-credential-lifecycle/src/{chain,registry,operations}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-credential-lifecycle/tests/chain*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/credential/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-webauthn-verify`, `mc-wire`, `mc-authority-db`, `mc-test-support`.

### W01-T029-RUNNER-CONTROL-SERVER
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T029-RUNNER-CONTROL-SERVER
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the real Runner Control server: the mutually authenticated (mTLS) HTTPS API runners use. It negotiates versions first, then uses leases for every action. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-runner-control` (I-09 real): the mTLS HTTP server for session/poll/accept/heartbeat/complete/receipts per T003, using `mc-negotiation` first and `mc-lease` for every lease-bound route.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** The runner's only authority ingress; P0-08A and P0-06D run through it.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 10 Runner Control ingress; 20; 07/29; INV-13, INV-18.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-08A, P0-06D.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** I-09 spec; `mc-lease`, `mc-negotiation`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** server binary `mc-runner-control`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** binary `mc-runner-control --config <file>`; `pub struct RunnerControlConfig { listen, server_cert, server_key, client_ca, identity_map }` from file/env; routes exactly per `doubles/runner-control/v1/openapi.yaml`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-runner-control/src/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-runner-control/tests/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-lease`, `mc-negotiation`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T014 INTEGRATED; W01-T023 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-runner-control/src/lib.rs (owned)`; `crates/mc-runner-control/src/{server.rs,tls.rs,routes.rs,identity.rs,config.rs}`; `crates/mc-runner-control/src/main.rs`; `crates/mc-runner-control/tests/{e2e.rs,negative.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. rustls server requiring client certs; cert → runner identity mapping from config. 2. Session route runs negotiation; all other routes require the pinned set digest. 3. Lease routes call `mc-lease`; no DB credentials leave the server. 4. Test certs generated with rcgen in tests.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** No authority before negotiation; unauthenticated requests rejected.

Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** No client cert; wrong pinned digest; heartbeat after DB expiry.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Spec conformant; all negatives rejected.

### W01-T030-EFFECT-DISPATCH
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T030-EFFECT-DISPATCH
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Join the effects database and the journal into the complete "send exactly once" protocol, and prove it behaves correctly at each of the 6 crash points the architecture lists. **Precisely:** Implement the full I-04 `EffectAuthority` by joining the effects store (T016) and journal (T017): create_pending → prepare (journal PREPARED with both receipts) → start → arm → send → observe (journal OBSERVED, then DB projection) → mark_unknown; plus the crash-matrix tests, one per row of doc 50.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-023: the complete dispatch protocol other workstreams integrate with at IM-3.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 50 dispatch protocol steps 1–6 and crash matrix (6 rows); 29 effect dispatch; INV-01, INV-13, INV-17.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09G/H, P0-08D, P0-03C/E, P0-10G/H.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc-effects` store, `mc-journal`; ProviderResultV1.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `EffectAuthority` implementation (I-04@v1 real). **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct PgEffectAuthority { store: EffectStore, journal: Arc<dyn JournalSequencer> }` implementing `EffectAuthority` (I-04@v1 real).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-effects/src/{dispatch,authority}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-effects/tests/{dispatch,crash_matrix}*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-effects` store module, `mc-journal`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T016 INTEGRATED; W01-T017 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-effects/src/{dispatch.rs,authority.rs}`; `crates/mc-effects/tests/{dispatch.rs,crash_matrix.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Ordering PREPARED → STARTED → SEND_ARMED → send → OBSERVED, never reordered. 2. Dispatch deadline expiry denies send; request deadline marks UNKNOWN. 3. One test per crash-matrix row (6), each asserting the documented recovery meaning.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** No send without both receipts; no replacement send claim; UNKNOWN never becomes absence.
- **FAILURE CASES:** DB projection before journal OBSERVED.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** 6 crash-matrix tests; deadline tests. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `dispatch::full_order_prepared_started_armed_sent_observed`; `dispatch::observed_journaled_before_db_projection`; `dispatch::dispatch_window_expiry_denies_send`; `dispatch::request_deadline_marks_unknown`; `dispatch::ack_loss_at_prepared_do_not_send`; `dispatch::ack_loss_at_send_armed_unknown`; `dispatch::witness_partition_do_not_send`; `dispatch::used_claim_replay_rejected`; `crash_matrix::row1_before_prepared`; `crash_matrix::row2_prepared_no_start`; `crash_matrix::row3_started_no_arm`; `crash_matrix::row4_armed_send_unknown`; `crash_matrix::row5_observed_db_lost`; `crash_matrix::row6_restore_lost_operation`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Ack loss at PREPARED and at SEND_ARMED; witness partition; replay of a used claim.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Zero sends without two durable receipts in every injected case.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Crash-matrix report. A1 sends INTERFACE_NOTICE I-04@v1 (IM-3 ready on the W01 side).

### W01-T031-GATE-COMMIT
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T031-GATE-COMMIT
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the gate: it locks everything in a fixed order, re-checks that every input is still current, asks the reducer for the verdict and signs it. A revocation can never slip in between. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-gate-authority` (I-05 part 2): the gate commit transaction (lock in doc 29 order, recheck subject/policy/trust/revocation generations, evaluation sequence, manifest and receipt roots; call the I-06 reducer with the DB time sample; insert immutable decision; CAS current pointer; outbox referencing GateProofV2), the signer that re-verifies provenance from the DB, and the revocation path on the same locks.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-019: revocation and gate commit serialize; the signer never trusts API input.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 06 gate commitment; 29 lock order; 51 transaction and reducer; INV-03, INV-06.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-07B; P0-07A/C/E (W02).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** GateProofV2, EvaluationIdentityV2, PolicyVersionV1, TrustBundleV1, RevocationRecordV1, VerificationSetV2; I-06 reducer (W02 double `ws02/doubles/reducer-table/v1` until W02-T009 INTEGRATED).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** GateAuthority implementation; migrations `db/authority/gate/*`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct PgGate { pool, signer: GateSigner, reducer: Box<dyn Fn(&GateInputs, i64) -> GateDecision + Send + Sync> }` implementing `GateAuthority`; the reducer is injected (W02 crate or its double), never reimplemented here; `async fn resolve_inputs(&self, tx, evaluation_id) -> Result<GateInputs, GateError>` loads and digest-validates every resolved view of W02's `GateInputs`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-gate-authority/src/**` (except `api.rs`), `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-gate-authority/tests/**`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/gate/**`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-ingestion`, `mc-wire`, `mc-authority-db`, `doubles/lease/v1`, W02 reducer double, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T019 INTEGRATED; W01-T015 INTEGRATED; W01-T052 INTEGRATED; W02-T004 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-gate-authority/src/lib.rs (add modules)`; `crates/mc-gate-authority/src/{commit.rs,signer.rs,revocation.rs,reducer_port.rs}`; `db/authority/gate/0001_decisions_pointer.sql`; `db/authority/gate/0002_grants.sql`; `crates/mc-gate-authority/tests/{commit.rs,race.rs,negative.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Commit transaction as above, no network call under locks. 2. **Resolve and validate the reducer's inputs** (audit F-5): load the locked current PolicyVersionV1 and its requirements, the AnalysisUnitV1 entries of the sealed plan, the coverage descriptor named by each receipt's `coverage_digest`, the FindingCandidateV2 records with their oracle trust classes, and every VerificationReceiptV2 named by the VerificationSetV2 `receipt_digests`; recompute each canonical digest and refuse (no decision) on any mismatch; pass them as the resolved views of W02's `GateInputs` (W02-T004). The reducer does no IO. 3. Signer reloads inputs from the DB and recomputes the commitment. 4. Revocation: same locks; appends STALE/REVOKED currentness and a withdrawal intent.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-03, INV-06; no caller-selected verdict.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Signer signs an API-supplied digest; revocation and gate both commit.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Positive path → PASS proof (test key); 1,000-iteration interleaving of revocation and commit with exactly one consistent order. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `commit::legitimate_receipts_produce_pass_proof`; `commit::lock_order_matches_doc29 (asserted via lock trace)`; `commit::signer_reloads_inputs_from_db`; `race::revocation_vs_commit_1000_iterations_single_order`; `race::no_pass_after_revocation`; `negative::stale_policy_generation`; `negative::revoked_analyzer_after_completion_stale_and_withdrawal`; `negative::substituted_trust_set_digest`; `negative::api_supplied_digest_ignored`; `resolve::coverage_descriptor_digest_mismatch_no_decision`; `resolve::verification_member_missing_no_pass`; `resolve::reducer_receives_only_validated_views`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Stale policy generation; revoked analyzer; substituted trust-set digest; a coverage descriptor whose digest differs from the receipt's `coverage_digest` → no decision; a verification member missing from the loaded set → no PASS.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** No PASS proof committed after revocation in the race test; the reducer only ever receives digest-validated resolved views.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Race statistics. A1 sends INTERFACE_NOTICE I-05@v1.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Reducer (W02); harness (T040).
- **BLOCKERS:** none (reducer double).
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Lock order; signer independence.

### W01-T032-AA-FINISH
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T032-AA-FINISH
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the second half of approval: verify the person's fingerprint/security-key response, record the approval, and make sure each approval is used for exactly one publication. **Precisely:** Implement the `finish` half of `mc-approval-authority`: in one transaction lock challenge/binding/user-tenant authority/plan currentness; verify freshness/one-use and the ceremony via `mc-webauthn-verify`; check credential state/scope/generation (CredentialRegistry API) and eligibility; insert the immutable HumanApprovalAssertionV2; consume the challenge. Plus `consume_assertion` binding the nonce to exactly one publication ID, and counter-anomaly suspension.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-018 / INV-10/11: only the exact current plan-purpose assertion qualifies, once.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 52 ceremony and transaction; ENCODING WebAuthn; INV-10, INV-11.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09C.

- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `ApprovalAuthority::{finish, consume_assertion}`; route `POST /aa/v1/challenges/{id}/finish`; migrations `db/authority/approval/0003_*`+. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** writes `impl ApprovalAuthority for PgApproval` (prepare delegates to W01-T025's inherent method; finish and consume_assertion implemented here); axum route `POST /aa/v1/challenges/:id/finish`. Tests use the real `PgCredentials` from W01-T028.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-approval-authority/src/{finish,consume,anomaly,http_finish}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-approval-authority/tests/finish*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/approval/0003_*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/approval/0004_*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** T025 modules, `mc-webauthn-verify`, `mc-credential-lifecycle::api`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T025 INTEGRATED; W01-T009 INTEGRATED; W01-T028 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-approval-authority/src/{finish.rs,consume.rs,anomaly.rs,http_finish.rs}`; `db/authority/approval/0003_assertions_nonces.sql`; `db/authority/approval/0004_grants.sql`; `crates/mc-approval-authority/tests/finish.rs`. **Steps:** 1. Verification order per 52. 2. Replay of identical bytes is idempotent; altered reuse rejected. 3. Counter anomaly → suspend the binding.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** A WebAuthn signature alone is never a display receipt; ManualResolution-purpose ceremonies never accepted for publication.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Skipping UV; nonce consumed twice under concurrency.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Full prepare → finish → consume with the software authenticator; 1,000-iteration concurrent consume → one publication. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `finish::prepare_finish_consume_happy_path`; `finish::wrong_rp_id`; `finish::wrong_origin`; `finish::uv_absent`; `finish::expired_challenge`; `finish::reused_nonce`; `finish::plan_changed_between_prepare_and_finish`; `finish::resolution_purpose_assertion_rejected`; `finish::counter_anomaly_suspends_binding`; `finish::identical_replay_idempotent`; `finish::altered_reuse_rejected`; `finish::concurrent_consume_1000_iterations_one_publication`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Wrong RP/origin; absent UV; expired challenge; reused nonce; plan changed between prepare and finish; resolution-purpose assertion.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Exactly-once consumption; every altered ceremony rejects.

### W01-T034-P0-09F-HARNESS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T034-P0-09F-HARNESS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the test program ("harness") that will later prove claim P0-09F: "Patch custody exact byte lifetime". It runs one normal case and then 5 attack/failure cases, each from a clean start, and writes the evidence files. In this task it only runs locally against fakes (DRY_RUN); the real run is the separate RUN task after G2/G3. **Precisely:** Build the P0-09F harness: verify → approve → publish → reconcile with custody; attempt deletion at each intermediate state; substitute an object; lose the runner workspace; corrupt a storage digest; premature retention expiry; revoke a grant.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** P0-09F proves custody keeps exact bytes through all phases.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 14; 24; ADR-025; INV-11, INV-16, INV-19.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** P0-09F (primary).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc-custody`; approval/publication steps simulated through api traits.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** harness emitting `custody-pin-history.json`, `artifact-digests.json`, `publication-tree-comparison.json`; `run-manifest.draft.json`. **Evidence files the harness writes:** `custody-pin-history.json`, `artifact-digests.json`, `publication-tree-comparison.json`, `P0-09F/run-manifest.json`, `P0-09F/fixture-digests.json`, `P0-09F/evidence-manifest.sha256`, `P0-09F/independent-review.json` (run-manifest/evidence-manifest/independent-review are finalized only in the RUN task).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-09F/**` (except `harness/Cargo.toml`)
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-custody`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T021 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `claims/P0-09F/harness/src/main.rs`; `claims/P0-09F/harness/src/{scenarios.rs,report.rs}`; `claims/P0-09F/fixtures/** (synthetic only)`; `claims/P0-09F/fixture-digests.json`; `claims/P0-09F/run-manifest.draft.json`; `claims/P0-09F/README.md (how to run DRY_RUN; which injections; expected outputs)`. **Steps:** 1. Scenario runner over the states. 2. Each injection from a reset baseline. 3. Emit expected vs observed. **Claim procedure the harness must automate (verbatim from atomic-prototype-claims.json P0-09F):** (1) Provision exactly the sealed cell and empty evidence directory named P0-09F. (2) Verify, approve, publish and reconcile exact bytes; attempt deletion at each intermediate state. (3) Run each semicolon-separated injected case separately from reset baseline; retain both control and failure traces. (4) Evaluate the declared observations and thresholds; reviewer signs evidence manifest and declares PASS, FAIL or INCONCLUSIVE.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-11.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Positive control missing.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** DRY_RUN of all injections. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `scenarios::positive_control`; `scenarios::injection_a_substitute_object`; `scenarios::injection_b_lose_local_runner_workspace`; `scenarios::injection_c_storage_digest_corruption`; `scenarios::injection_d_premature_retention_expiry`; `scenarios::injection_e_revoke_grant`; `report::rerun_is_byte_identical`; `report::every_case_has_expected_and_observed`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** All P0-09F injections; cross-tenant read with a matching hash. **Claim injections — each one a separate scenario from a reset baseline, keeping control and failure traces:** (a) Substitute object; (b) lose local runner workspace; (c) storage digest corruption; (d) premature retention expiry; (e) revoke grant.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Substituted or missing bytes block authority; deletion only after allowed terminal reconciliation. **Claim expected observations:** Substitution/missing bytes blocks authority; pinned exact bytes survive required phases; deletion releases only after allowed terminal reconciliation. **Threshold:** Zero unauthorized acceptances, effects or disclosure across all listed cases; every legitimate control succeeds. **DRY_RUN must show:** positive control succeeds and every injection produces the expected observation.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** DRY_RUN outputs SHA-256.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Claim execution.
- **BLOCKERS:** none.

### W01-T035-RESOLUTION-FLOWS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T035-RESOLUTION-FLOWS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Implement the seven kinds of human resolution (for example "this finding is a false positive" or "we cannot tell whether this call ran"), each with its separation-of-duty rule. **Precisely:** Implement the seven resolution flows of doc 56 on the T027 core (untrusted oracle PROVIDE_ORACLE, disputed finding, false-positive exclusion, unsupported C3, UNKNOWN effect with exact positive result, claimed non-execution, unresolvable uncertainty), the separation rules, CLOSED_UNRESOLVED tombstones and "fresh evaluation requested" where 56 requires.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** P0-10J and W02's oracle approval (P0-07C) need the flows.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 56 flow table; 13 maintainer-oracle protocol; 19 Manual Reconciliation; INV-22.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-10J, P0-07C, P0-09H.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** ManualResolutionV1, OracleTrustClassV1, EffectStateV1 (via `mc-effects::api`).
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** flow handlers; I-08@v1 complete. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** seven `FlowHandler` implementations (trait from W01-T027 `commit.rs`) and `pub fn register_all(reg: &mut FlowRegistry)` in `flows/mod.rs`; `FlowOutcome { Resolved, ClosedUnresolved, FreshEvaluationRequested }` is defined in W01-T027 `commit.rs` (no PASS).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-resolution/src/flows/**`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-resolution/tests/flows*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** T027 modules, `mc-effects`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T027 INTEGRATED; W01-T016 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-resolution/src/flows/{mod.rs,provide_oracle.rs,disputed_finding.rs,false_positive.rs,unsupported_c3.rs,unknown_positive.rs,non_execution.rs,unresolvable.rs}`; `crates/mc-resolution/tests/flows.rs`. **Steps:** 1. One handler per flow. 2. Collector ≠ terminal non-execution reviewer; the author of disputed evidence cannot validate it. 3. CLOSED_UNRESOLVED keeps tombstones and never frees a dedupe key.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-22; no PASS.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Same actor bypasses dual control.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** One test per flow (7). **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `flows::provide_oracle`; `flows::disputed_finding`; `flows::false_positive_exclusion`; `flows::unsupported_c3`; `flows::unknown_effect_exact_positive`; `flows::claimed_non_execution`; `flows::unresolvable_closes_with_tombstone`; `flows::collector_cannot_review_non_execution`; `flows::evidence_author_cannot_validate_dispute`; `flows::conflicting_provider_proof_rejected`; `flows::terminal_state_not_resurrected`; `flows::closed_unresolved_keeps_dedupe_key`; `flows::approval_recovery_kind_rejected_not_authorized`; `flows::pair_not_in_table_rejected`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Separation violations; conflicting provider proof; terminal-state resurrection.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** 7 flows pass; separation enforced; tombstones persist.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report. A1 sends INTERFACE_NOTICE I-08@v1.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Harness (T044); UI (W05).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Separation rules.

### W01-T036-CREDENTIAL-OPERATIONS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T036-CREDENTIAL-OPERATIONS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Implement every approver-key operation (add, replace, revoke, suspend, recover with a 24-hour hold, and so on) with its rules about who must agree, and stop the emergency "break glass" role from ever approving. **Precisely:** Implement the operation matrix of `mc-credential-lifecycle`: INITIAL_BOOTSTRAP, INITIAL_REGISTRATION, ADD, REPLACE, REVOKE, SUSPEND, RECOVER (24 h hold with notices and recheck at hold completion) and CHANGE_BINDING under DUAL_ADMIN and INDIVIDUAL_OFFLINE; break glass limited to suspend/revoke/initiate recovery (G-042).
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** SEC-01: ordinary login recovery never restores approval authority.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 52 "Operation-specific authorization matrix"; 15; 48; ApprovalRecoveryPolicyV1; INV-10.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09B.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** Approval* lifecycle contracts.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** operation handlers. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** extends `PgCredentials::apply` to all 8 operations × 2 profiles; `pub struct LifecycleConfig { pub recover_hold_h: u32 /* 24 per doc 52 */, pub case_lifetime_days: u32 /* 7 */, pub challenge_ttl_s: u32 /* 300 */ }`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-credential-lifecycle/src/{operations,recovery,break_glass}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-credential-lifecycle/tests/operations*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** T028 modules, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T028 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-credential-lifecycle/src/{operations.rs,recovery.rs,break_glass.rs}`; `crates/mc-credential-lifecycle/tests/operations.rs`. **Steps:** 1. Distinct-person quorum; scope subset for ADD/REPLACE; expansions need a second admin. 2. REVOKE has no create ceremony; SUSPEND restoration needs a new generation. 3. RECOVER hold (duration from config = 24 h per 52), recheck at completion, 7-day case lifetime that cannot extend a 5-min challenge. 4. Offline keys are bootstrap/recovery only, never publication approvers. 5. Break-glass assertion attempt → denied.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-10.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Same person counted twice; hold bypass.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** One positive path per operation × profile (8 × 2) with a controllable clock. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `operations::<op>_<profile> for op in initial_bootstrap, initial_registration, add, replace, revoke, suspend, recover, change_binding and profile in dual_admin, individual_offline (16 tests)`; `operations::same_person_twice_in_quorum_rejected`; `operations::scope_expansion_via_add_rejected`; `operations::expansion_needs_second_admin`; `operations::revoke_has_no_create_ceremony`; `operations::suspend_restore_needs_new_generation`; `operations::recover_hold_recheck_at_completion`; `operations::case_lifetime_cannot_extend_challenge`; `operations::offline_key_cannot_approve_publication`; `operations::wrong_role_offline_signature`; `operations::break_glass_cannot_mint_assertion`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Scope expansion via ADD; wrong-role offline signature; break-glass assertion; quorum with one person twice.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Every matrix rule enforced.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Harness (T045); production IdP/KMS.
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Distinct-person counting; hold recheck.

### W01-T037-EPOCH-FENCE-DENY
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T037-EPOCH-FENCE-DENY
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the first part of post-restore safety: after a database restore, move to a new security epoch and refuse every permission that came from the restored backup. **Precisely:** Implement part 1 of `mc-bootstrap`: epoch rotation (append JournalEpochFenceV1 with old-sender fencing evidence; equality-only epoch comparison) and the restored-authority deny filter keyed by the old epoch for every entitlement type (keys, authenticator bindings, policy, trust/analyzer permissions, memberships, roles, eligibility).
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** DS-01: restore must not resurrect grants whose revocation was lost.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 35 steps 1–6; 48 rollback containment; 50 restore; INV-10, INV-13.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-10I.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** JournalEpochFenceV1, TrustBundleV1, ApprovalCredentialBindingV1, RevocationRecordV1.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** EpochFence, RestoredAuthorityFilter; migrations `db/authority/bootstrap/0001_*`. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct JournalEpochFenceImpl { journal: Arc<dyn JournalSequencer> }` implementing `EpochFence`; `pub struct EpochDenyFilter { current_epoch: Uuid }` implementing `RestoredAuthorityFilter` (equality-only epoch comparison).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-bootstrap/src/{fence,deny}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-bootstrap/tests/{fence,deny}*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/bootstrap/0001_*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-journal`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T017 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-bootstrap/src/{fence.rs,deny.rs}`; `db/authority/bootstrap/0001_epochs.sql`; `crates/mc-bootstrap/tests/{fence.rs,deny.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Fence append via the journal. 2. Deny filter consulted by every authority check of each entitlement type (exposed as one function). 3. Tombstones and consumed send claims are never cleared.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** Zero restored entitlements accepted.
- **FAILURE CASES:** One entitlement type missing from the filter.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** One denial test per entitlement type (7). **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `fence::rotation_appends_fence_with_evidence`; `fence::old_sender_fenced`; `deny::signing_key_denied`; `deny::authenticator_binding_denied`; `deny::policy_denied`; `deny::trust_or_analyzer_permission_denied`; `deny::membership_denied`; `deny::role_denied`; `deny::eligibility_denied`; `deny::tombstones_and_used_claims_survive`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Old-epoch key/authenticator replay under the new epoch.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** All 7 types denied post-fence.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Fresh bootstrap flow (T046).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Deny-by-default completeness.

### W01-T042-PUBLICATION-PLANNER

Test asserts the DB role cannot write plan/approval/effect rows.

### W01-T043-GIT-GATEWAY
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T043-GIT-GATEWAY
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the Git gateway: the only component holding the GitHub publication token. It sends exactly five allowed kinds of request (blob, tree, commit, new branch, pull request) and refuses everything else before any network call. **Precisely:** Implement `mc-git-gateway`: the independent Git Publication Capability Gateway holding the staging Publication App token. It builds exactly the five allowed GitHub requests from the verified plan (POST git/blobs, git/trees, git/commits, git/refs `refs/heads/mastercoder/fix/<publication_id>`, pulls), rechecks per-effect authority (I-04 STARTED, live lease, approval, binding, eligibility, custody, freeze), requires returned-OID/tree equality between steps, and denies everything else before dial.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** ADR-016 (finding 1): create-only is enforced by an independent gateway, not token permissions.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 53 in full (closed vocabulary, denied operations, TLS/origin/redirect rules); 27; 30; INV-04, INV-12, INV-17.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09D, P0-09G, P0-09H.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** GitPublicationCapabilityV2, EffectPlanEntryV1, Create*RequestV1, HumanApprovalAssertionV2, EligibilityProofV2, I-04, I-16; I-12 fake GitHub (W02) for BUILD.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** Gateway implementation + binary; migrations `db/authority/publication/0003_*` (capability nonces). **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub struct GitGateway { http: reqwest::Client /* redirects disabled, no retries */, effects: Arc<dyn EffectAuthority>, plans, approvals, custody, cfg: GatewayConfig }` implementing `Gateway`; `pub enum AllowedOp { CreateBlob, CreateTree, CreateCommit, CreateRef, CreateFixPr }` (closed, 5 variants); `pub struct GatewayConfig { pub api_base: Url, pub token_path: PathBuf }` from config.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-git-gateway/src/**` (except `api.rs` and `reconcile*`), `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-git-gateway/tests/**` (except `reconcile*`), `ws01-core-security/db/authority/publication/0003_*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-effects`, `mc-plan-verifier`, `mc-approval-authority`, `mc-custody`, `ws02/doubles/fake-github/v1`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T030 INTEGRATED; W01-T033 INTEGRATED; W01-T032 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-git-gateway/src/lib.rs (add modules except reconcile)`; `crates/mc-git-gateway/src/{gateway.rs,vocabulary.rs,authority.rs,client.rs,equality.rs,config.rs}`; `crates/mc-git-gateway/src/main.rs`; `db/authority/publication/0003_capability_nonces.sql`; `crates/mc-git-gateway/tests/{allowed.rs,denied.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Resolve owner/name from the stable repository ID. 2. Bodies from custody only; no redirects with credentials; SDK auto-retry disabled for mutations. 3. Per effect: one-use capability nonce `(tenant_id, capability_id)`, then I-04 prepare/start/arm/send/observe. 4. Equality chain: blob/tree OIDs → CREATE_COMMIT only after tree equality → CREATE_REF after commit tree/parent equality → CREATE_FIX_PR after ref confirmed.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-04, INV-12; no raw HTTP passthrough, GraphQL or arbitrary headers.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Ref update via fast-forward; caller-supplied path reaching dial; redirect forwarding the token.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Full allowed sequence against fake GitHub; each denial before any dial (request counter = 0). **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `allowed::full_sequence_against_fake_github`; `allowed::ref_name_is_mastercoder_fix_publication_id`; `denied::merge_pre_dial`; `denied::patch_existing_ref_pre_dial`; `denied::force_update_pre_dial`; `denied::delete_pre_dial`; `denied::default_branch_mutation_pre_dial`; `denied::contents_api_pre_dial`; `denied::arbitrary_url_body_repository_pre_dial`; `denied::bypass_flag_pre_dial`; `denied::tree_mismatch_stops_commit`; `denied::renamed_repository_identity`; `denied::capability_reused`; `denied::redirect_not_followed_with_token`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Merge, PATCH existing ref, force update, delete, default/source branch mutation, Contents API, arbitrary URL/body/repository, bypass flag; tree mismatch stops commit; renamed repository identity.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Every forbidden operation denied pre-dial; allowed plan creates only the fixed-namespace ref/PR.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report with request counters.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Reconciliation (T047); harnesses (T048/T049).
- **BLOCKERS:** none (fake GitHub).
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Closed vocabulary truly closed; pre-dial denial.

One case per flow. 2. Injections from reset baselines. 3. Emit results. **Claim procedure the harness must automate (verbatim from atomic-prototype-claims.json P0-10J):** (1) Provision exactly the sealed cell and empty evidence directory named P0-10J. (2) Attempt allowed resolution with proof, then verify resulting state plus any fresh approval/re-evaluation required. (3) Run each semicolon-separated injected case separately from reset baseline; retain both control and failure traces. - **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Only scoped, proof-backed transitions; no PASS. **Claim expected observations:** Only scoped proof-backed transitions occur; ambiguity not laundered into retry or PASS; fresh state keeps lineage and invalidates old authority. **Threshold:** Zero unauthorized acceptances, effects or disclosure across all listed cases; every legitimate control succeeds. **DRY_RUN must show:** positive control succeeds and every injection produces the expected observation.

It runs one normal case and then 4 attack/failure cases, each from a clean start, and writes the evidence files. In this task it only runs locally against fakes (DRY_RUN); the real run is the separate RUN task after G2/G3. **Precisely:** Build the P0-09B harness using the W05 test IdP (W05-T007) for ordinary account recovery: recover the ordinary account, then try enrollment/replacement; forged registrar; add a credential using the ordinary session only; replay recovery; revoke while a challenge is live.

Drive the IdP recovery flow. 2. Attempt each lifecycle action with only the ordinary session. 3. Emit lineage and denials. **Claim procedure the harness must automate (verbatim from atomic-prototype-claims.json P0-09B):** (1) Provision exactly the sealed cell and empty evidence directory named P0-09B. (2) Recover ordinary account, try enrollment/replacement, then complete valid separately authorized approval recovery. (3) Run each semicolon-separated injected case separately from reset baseline; retain both control and failure traces. (4) Evaluate the declared observations and thresholds; reviewer signs evidence manifest and declares PASS, FAIL or INCONCLUSIVE.

- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** All P0-09B injections; break-glass assertion attempt. **Claim injections — each one a separate scenario from a reset baseline, keeping control and failure traces:** (a) Forged registrar; (b) add credential using ordinary session only; (c) replay recovery; (d) revoke credential while challenge live.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Ordinary recovery grants zero approval power. **Claim expected observations:** Ordinary recovery grants no approval power; fresh bound registration and scope/credential generation enforced. **Threshold:** Zero unauthorized acceptances, effects or disclosure across all listed cases; every legitimate control succeeds. **DRY_RUN must show:** positive control succeeds and every injection produces the expected observation.

### W01-T046-FRESH-BOOTSTRAP-FLOW
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T046-FRESH-BOOTSTRAP-FLOW
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the second part of post-restore safety: new permissions can only come from a fresh, independently approved bootstrap using offline recovery keys and current proof of GitHub ownership. **Precisely:** Implement part 2 of `mc-bootstrap`: the fresh bootstrap flow requiring offline recovery root signatures plus current provider-ownership evidence, producing a new inventory digest (no bulk copy, no re-signing), and the unfreeze decision record requiring independent reviewer IDs.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** DS-01: new authority only from a complete, independently approved bootstrap.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 35 steps 6–9; INV-10, INV-13.

- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-bootstrap/src/{bootstrap,unfreeze}*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-bootstrap/tests/{bootstrap,unfreeze}*`, `ws01-core-security/db/authority/bootstrap/0002_*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** T037 modules, `mc-credential-lifecycle`, `mc-test-support`.

- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-bootstrap/src/{bootstrap.rs,unfreeze.rs}`; `db/authority/bootstrap/0002_inventory_unfreeze.sql`; `crates/mc-bootstrap/tests/{bootstrap.rs,unfreeze.rs}`. **Steps:** 1. Inventory built only from fresh evidence. 2. Missing root or ownership proof → refuse. 3. Unfreeze requires reviewer IDs distinct from the operator.

### W01-T047-GATEWAY-RECONCILE
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T047-GATEWAY-RECONCILE
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build reconciliation for the gateway: when a GitHub call's outcome is unknown, find out exactly what happened. "Not found" is never treated as proof that nothing happened. **Precisely:** Implement the reconciliation module of `mc-git-gateway`: exact reads of object/ref/PR identity plus provenance; an existing deterministic ref with the expected OID is reconciled as the prior effect; conflicting contents → MANUAL_RECONCILIATION; not-found is never proof of absence.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Finding 11: ambiguity must stay UNKNOWN until exact proof.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 50 outcomes and replacement proof; 53 existing-ref reconciliation; 56 UNKNOWN cases; INV-17.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** supports P0-09H.
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** ProviderResultV1, EffectStateV1.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** `reconcile` module. **Exact public API (names and signatures are binding; do not rename):** `pub async fn reconcile(&self, tenant_id: Uuid, effect_id: Uuid) -> Result<ReconcileOutcome, GatewayDenial>`; `pub enum ReconcileOutcome { MatchesPriorEffect, StillUnknown, ManualReconciliation }` (no variant means 'absent').
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-git-gateway/src/reconcile*`, `ws01-core-security/crates/mc-git-gateway/tests/reconcile*`
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** T043 modules, `ws02/doubles/fake-github/v1` (not-found-then-present mode).
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T043 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `crates/mc-git-gateway/src/reconcile.rs`; `crates/mc-git-gateway/tests/reconcile.rs`. **Steps:** 1. Exact identity read. 2. Expected OID → reconcile; different → MANUAL_RECONCILIATION; not-found → stay UNKNOWN. 3. Late result binds the original intent.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-17.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Retry after not-found.
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** Not-found-then-present sequence. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `reconcile::expected_oid_reconciled_as_prior_effect`; `reconcile::not_found_stays_unknown_no_retry`; `reconcile::not_found_then_present_binds_original_intent`; `reconcile::conflicting_ref_manual_reconciliation`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** Not-found → no retry; conflicting ref → MANUAL_RECONCILIATION.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Never treats not-found as absence.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** Test report.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** Harnesses.
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Proof predicates.
