### W01-T050-P0-10I-HARNESS
- **TASK_ID:** W01-T050-P0-10I-HARNESS
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Build the test program ("harness") that will later prove claim P0-10I: "No authority resurrection after restore". It runs one normal case and then 4 attack/failure cases, each from a clean start, and writes the evidence files. In this task it only runs locally against fakes (DRY_RUN); the real run is the separate RUN task after G2/G3. **Precisely:** Build the P0-10I harness: snapshot with live lease, success, unused nonce, old credential binding, trust bundle; revoke K/A and remove M after backup B; restore B omitting all post-backup revocations; fence old egress; verify the journal; inject bulk re-sign, replay of old authenticator/workload key under the new epoch, missing root/ownership proof.
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** P0-10I proves post-restore containment.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 35 step 6; 48; INV-10, INV-13.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** P0-10I (primary).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** `mc-bootstrap`, `mc-journal`.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** harness emitting `fresh-bootstrap-inventory.json`, `replay-denial-matrix.json`, `unfreeze-decision.json`; `run-manifest.draft.json`. **Evidence files the harness writes:** `fresh-bootstrap-inventory.json`, `replay-denial-matrix.json`, `unfreeze-decision.json`, `P0-10I/run-manifest.json`, `P0-10I/fixture-digests.json`, `P0-10I/evidence-manifest.sha256`, `P0-10I/independent-review.json` (run-manifest/evidence-manifest/independent-review are finalized only in the RUN task).
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-10I/**` (except `harness/Cargo.toml`)
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** `mc-bootstrap`, `mc-journal`, `mc-test-support`.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** W01-T046 INTEGRATED.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** **Files to create/edit (paths under `ws01-core-security/`):** `claims/P0-10I/harness/src/main.rs`; `claims/P0-10I/harness/src/{scenarios.rs,report.rs}`; `claims/P0-10I/fixtures/** (synthetic only)`; `claims/P0-10I/fixture-digests.json`; `claims/P0-10I/run-manifest.draft.json`; `claims/P0-10I/README.md (how to run DRY_RUN; which injections; expected outputs)`. **Steps:** 1. Local backup/restore of a test DB (pg_dump/pg_restore). 2. Injections from reset baselines. 3. Emit results. **Claim procedure the harness must automate (verbatim from atomic-prototype-claims.json P0-10I):** (1) Create trusted analyzer key K, approver credential A, policy P and membership M; take business-DB backup B. (2) Revoke K/A and remove M after B, then restore B while deliberately omitting every post-backup revocation record. Fence old sender egress and verify complete effect journal. (3) Attempt fresh-epoch bulk re-sign/reuse of restored K/A/P/M; each must reject before authoritative evaluation or send. (4) Perform independently reviewed bootstrap with offline recovery roots and fresh current provider ownership, new keys/authenticators, explicit current policy/analyzer approval; only this new inventory may admit work. (5) Remove the offline recovery-root proof or ownership proof in separate reset runs; verify privileged admission stays frozen.
- **SECURITY INVARIANTS:** INV-10, INV-13.
- **FAILURE CASES:** Restore includes post-backup revocations (test invalid).
- **TESTS REQUIRED:** DRY_RUN. **Exact test functions (each must exist and pass; the RETURN lists them with results):** `scenarios::positive_control`; `scenarios::injection_a_lost_post_backup_revocations_with_restored_valid_old_signatu`; `scenarios::injection_b_bulk_re_sign_of_restored_grants_into_new_epoch`; `scenarios::injection_c_replay_old_authenticator_or_workload_key_under_new_epoch`; `scenarios::injection_d_missing_offline_recovery_root_or_current_owner_proof`; `report::rerun_is_byte_identical`; `report::every_case_has_expected_and_observed`. Always also: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy -p <crate> --all-targets -- -D warnings`.
- **NEGATIVE TESTS:** All P0-10I injections. **Claim injections — each one a separate scenario from a reset baseline, keeping control and failure traces:** (a) Lost post-backup revocations with restored valid old signatures; (b) Bulk re-sign of restored grants into new epoch; (c) Replay old authenticator or workload key under new epoch; (d) Missing offline recovery root or current owner proof.
- **ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:** Matches all four P0-10I expected observations. **Claim expected observations:** Zero restored entitlements accepted or re-signed automatically; Every old key/authenticator remains ineligible even if revocation history is absent; Only complete independently approved fresh bootstrap enables new authority; missing root/ownership remains frozen; Historical effect tombstones and consumed send claims survive bootstrap. **Threshold:** Zero unauthorized acceptances, effects or disclosure across all listed cases; every legitimate control succeeds. **DRY_RUN must show:** positive control succeeds and every injection produces the expected observation.
- **EVIDENCE TO RETURN:** DRY_RUN SHA-256s.
- **OUT-OF-SCOPE:** W06 restore drill (RUN via IM-7).
- **BLOCKERS:** none.
- **A3 REVIEW FOCUS:** Restore fidelity.

### W01-RUN-P0-06C
- **TASK_ID:** W01-RUN-P0-06C
- **OBJECTIVE:** **In plain words:** Run the finished test program for claim P0-06C ("Attempt and tenant domain separation") on the real staging setup, collect every evidence file, and hand the sealed bundle to two fresh reviewer sessions (each applying another lane's review playbook), who decide if it passed. Allowed only after G2 (external audit passed) and G3 (you authorized this claim family). **Precisely:** Execute atomic claim P0-06C — "Attempt and tenant domain separation" — for its exact sealed capability cell and submit evidence for independent two-lens acceptance (lifecycle NOT_RUN→RUNNING→SUBMITTED→ACCEPTED|REJECTED; outcome PASS|FAIL|INCONCLUSIVE kept separate).
- **WHY IT EXISTS:** Attempt and tenant domain separation; failure cannot be hidden by an umbrella gate result. Family gate P0-06. BLOCKS_FREEZE: YES.
- **ARCHITECTURE REQUIREMENTS:** 57 §P0-06C; TRACEABILITY/atomic-prototype-claims.json record P0-06C; 19 Prototype Evidence; 42; ENCODING signatures, CONTRACT_AUTHORITY, INV-02, INV-15.
- **P0 CLAIMS:** P0-06C (primary owner W01; status NOT_RUN).
- **INPUT CONTRACTS:** Harness from W01-T024. Topology: Fixtures differ only in tenant, repository, epoch, generation or canonical record domain. Fixture: P0-06C fixture as described in the procedure; fixtures committed and content-digested before execution. Configuration: One exact capability cell per execution; sealed run-manifest binds all settings, endpoint policies, limits, identity/permission/ruleset data and fixture digests. No inherited unspecified default is permitted.
- **OUTPUT CONTRACTS:** Evidence artifacts (I-13 formats): `binding-mutation-vectors.json`, `signature-verification-results.json`, `P0-06C/run-manifest.json`, `P0-06C/fixture-digests.json`, `P0-06C/evidence-manifest.sha256`, `P0-06C/independent-review.json`; `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/runs/<RUN_ID>/{run-manifest.json,outcome.json,lifecycle.jsonl,fixture-digests.json,reviews/}`; `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/current.json`.
- **EXACT WRITE SCOPE:** `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/**` (one new `runs/<RUN_ID>/` directory per execution plus `current.json`; earlier run directories are never modified); raw evidence only in the private evidence store under `evidence://P0-06C/default/<RUN_ID>/`.
- **READ-ONLY DEPENDENCIES:** — — each pinned at an INTEGRATED SHA recorded in the run-manifest; W06 evidence-kit; ARCHITECTURE/V2_1.
- **PRECONDITIONS:** G2 PASS; G3 signed for P0-06; W01-T024 INTEGRATED; integration milestone(s): —; Later explicit prototype authorization; disposable tenants/accounts and synthetic source only. No prototype execution is authorized by V2.1 architecture repair. Fixtures differ only in tenant, repository, epoch, generation or canonical record domain. Lock the exact run-manifest and fixture digests, record DB/provider observation clocks, name independent evidence reviewers and prove the positive control is executable before failure injection.
- **IMPLEMENTATION STEPS:** 1. Confirm G2 PASS and the signed G3 file for family P0-06 (put both SHA-256 in the run-manifest and in the RETURN MEMORY_UPDATES). 2. Allocate a new RUN_ID (`mc-evidence new-run`, UTC timestamp + random suffix) and create `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/runs/<RUN_ID>/`; never reuse or edit an earlier run directory. Seal `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/runs/<RUN_ID>/run-manifest.json` with `mc-evidence seal` (W06 I-13) per the pinning rule; record INTEGRATED SHAs of every participating component; refuse if any locally controlled version is missing. 3. Commit fixtures and `fixture-digests.json`; name both lens reviewers; prove the positive control is executable. 4. Provision exactly the sealed cell and empty evidence directory named P0-06C. 5. Generate signatures for baseline and attempt replay across each single-field mutation. 6. Run each semicolon-separated injected case separately from reset baseline; retain both control and failure traces. 7. Evaluate the declared observations and thresholds; reviewer signs evidence manifest and declares PASS, FAIL or INCONCLUSIVE. 8. Immediately after execution write the measured outcome PASS/FAIL/INCONCLUSIVE once to `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/runs/<RUN_ID>/outcome.json` (`mc-evidence outcome`; never edited afterwards, kept through rejection, expiry and invalidation — audit F-15). 9. Upload the complete bundle to the private evidence store under `evidence://P0-06C/default/<RUN_ID>/`; write `evidence-manifest.sha256`; append lifecycle SUBMITTED to `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/runs/<RUN_ID>/lifecycle.jsonl` and point `ws01-core-security/claims/P0-06C/cells/default/current.json` at this RUN_ID (`mc-evidence set-current`, recording the run it supersedes). 10. Send EVIDENCE_REVIEW_REQUEST to each lens reviewer; on two results append ACCEPTED or REJECTED to `lifecycle.jsonl`.
